総選挙で揺れるスペイン政治 
政権交代となれば欧州政治・経済にも影響

○ スペインでは総選挙を経て左派から右派へ政権が交代すると予想される。同国は寛容な移民政策
により高成長が続いてきたが住宅不足・価格高騰等の問題が生じ国民の不満が高まっている 
○ 選挙の最大の焦点も住宅や移民であることから新政権は住宅問題を解決すべく移民政策を厳格化
する可能性が高く、今後成長率は減速することが予想される 
○ また、右派政権が成立すればEU内の中道左派の影響力が低下し、移民政策や通商政策、対中政策
等の右傾化・保護主義化が進むことが想定される

１．スペインで前倒し総選挙を実施 
スペインでは11月29日、上下両院の選挙が実施される。2018年から首相を務めるサンチェス氏は、
もともと2027年までの議会任期を全うする方針を掲げていた。こうした中で状況を一変させたのが9月
に発生した大規模抗議活動だ。背景となったのは、住宅不足や家賃・住宅価格の高騰に対する不満だ。
9月下旬には、首都マドリードで長年暮らしてきた高齢女性が家賃の引き上げを巡る裁判で敗訴し、立
ち退きを余儀なくされた。女性が担架で運び出される映像は世間に衝撃を与え、住宅のアフォーダビ
リティ（取得・賃借のしやすさ）低下に対する不満が全国的な抗議活動へと広がった。 
政府は借家人保護の強化などを盛り込んだ緊急住宅問題対策を議会に提出したが、野党の反対によ

2

り否決された。その3日後の10月5日、サンチェス首相は「さらなる政策を実現するためには、国民の
支持を新たにし、拡大する必要がある」と述べ、議会の早期解散を表明した。 
早期解散の背景には、少数与党政権の議会運営の行き詰まりがあったとみられる。2023年に社会労
働党（PSOE）とSumarによる少数与党政権が成立して以降、政権はカタルーニャやバスクを中心とす
る地域政党の協力に頼ってきた。しかし、独立派政党の一つであるJuntsは2025年10月に政権への支持
を撤回した。このところ、予算の成立がままならない状況が続くなど政治が停滞する中で、同党の反
対により住宅問題対策が否決されたことは、政権が政策の実現に必要な議会の支持を確保しにくくな
っていることを改めて突き付けることとなった。 
また、野党への批判を梃子に議席を積み増す狙いも、このタイミングで解散に踏み切った背景にあ
ったとみられる。サンチェス首相は解散表明の演説で、「国家全体の利益を考える姿勢を欠いた野党
は、我が国の選挙制度の正当性を疑問視し、政策を妨害し、国にとって有益な施策に反対票を投じ続
けてきた」と述べた。さらには、野党を「スペインの民主主義が経験した中で最悪の野党」と厳しく
批判している。住宅問題への関心が高まるなか、「住宅問題の解決を目指す与党」と、「それを阻む
野党」という対立構造を明確に打ち出すことで、与党の支持回復につなげたいとの思惑がうかがえる。 
近年のスペインは左派連立政権のもとで高成長を遂げてきたが（図表1）、選挙を経て政策の方向性
が大きく変われば、同国経済への影響は避けられない。加えて、現サンチェス政権はEUの中で数少な
い中道左派政権であることを踏まえれば、選挙結果はEU全体の政策にも影響を及ぼすことが予想され
図表3 国民党、Vox、社会労働党の主な選挙公約

る。 
本稿では、選挙の見通しや主要政党の公約を整理したうえで、選挙結果がスペインおよびEU全体の
政治・経済に与える影響を考察する。 
２．右派への政権交代の公算大。住宅問題を受けて移民政策は転換へ 
選挙後は、中道左派・PSOEを中心とする左派連立政権から、中道右派・国民党（PP）が主導する右
派連立政権に交代し、PP党首のアルベルト・ヌニェス・フェイホー氏が首相に就任すると予想される。 
サンチェス首相率いるPSOEは議会第二党の座は維持するものの1、連立相手のSumarを母体とする左
派の選挙連合Frente Amplioの議席は大幅な減少が予想される（図表2）。一方で、現最大野党のPPは議
会第一党の座を維持する見通しだ。PPは単独過半数議席には届かないとみられ、議席の大幅な増加が
予想される極右・Voxとの連立政権を組成し政権奪取を試みることが見込まれる。 
PPが主導する右派連立政権が誕生する場合、どのような政策運営が行われるのだろうか。図表3には
主な公約をまとめている。まず、経済について、PPとVoxはVAT（付加価値税）や所得税、法人税の引
き下げ、行政手続き簡素化等を通じて家計や企業をサポートする姿勢を打ち出している。また、原発
も含めた技術中立的電源構成や電力関連税の引き下げにより、電気料金の抑制を図る構えだ。このほ
か、鉄道網の保守・更新をはじめとするインフラ投資を行うことで、今年に入って相次いでいる鉄道
事故により強まっている安全管理への批判に応えるとともに、交通・物流網の改善を通じて経済成長
にもつなげる方針だ。 
政権交代が生じる場合、大きな政策転換が予想されるのが移民政策だ。前述の通り、スペインは近
年高成長を謳歌しており、この背景にはサンチェス政権による寛容な移民政策があった。サンチェス
首相は、「移民は単なる人道主義の問題ではなく、経済の繁栄と福祉国家の持続可能性にとっても必
要」と発言するなど、移民受け入れを成長戦略と位置付けており、求職ビザの有効期間延長、家族呼
び寄せ要件の緩和、不法移民の大規模な合法化等を行ってきた。流入する移民の約6割が中南米のスペ
イン語圏出身者であり、言語や文化面での障壁が比較的小さいことも労働市場への参加を後押しした。

### Page 4

4

このため、スペイン出身の労働力人口が減少する中でも労働力人口全体は増加している（図表4）。労
働力人口が大幅に増加したことでインフレ下でも実質雇用者報酬はユーロ圏を上回る高い伸びが続き
（図表5）、消費を中心に経済が押し上げられたほか、労働投入の増加により潜在成長率も近年大幅に
向上している（図表6）。Banco de España （2026）は2022～2025年の経済成長のうち約5割が移民の流
入増加に起因すると分析している。 
もっとも、急速な移民流入は、社会インフラのひっ迫という問題も引き起こしている。中でも国民
の関心を集めているのが、議会解散のきっかけにもなった住宅問題だ。同国では、移民の流入による
世帯の増加に住宅供給が追い付かず、足元では約88万戸の住宅が不足していると計算される2（図表7）。
直近2025年の新規住宅供給は約8万戸であり、問題の深刻さがうかがえる。需給のひっ迫が住宅価格
（図表8）や家賃を押し上げ、住宅購入・賃借のハードル上昇や生活苦の要因になっている。実 際、ス
ペイン国立統計局の調査によれば、2025年時点で26～34歳の44.3%が親と同居しており、その47.3%が
住宅購入・賃借する経済的余裕のなさを理由に挙げている。自国が抱える課題を問う世論調査でも、
住宅や移民を挙げる割合が他の欧州主要国と比較して大幅に高い（ 図 表 9）。こうした世論を踏まえて、
PPやVoxはポイント制ビザの導入や不法移民の合法化禁止、受け入れ能力に合わせた移民受け入れ枠
の設置等により移民の流入数を抑え、住宅需要の抑制を目指している。同時に、規制緩和や建設増加
により住宅供給を増やすことで住宅を巡る有権者の不満を解消する構えだ。 
外交分野では、PPはEUや米国、中南米諸国との関係を重視する一方で、中国に対しては貿易不均衡
の是正などを念頭に厳しい姿勢で臨む方針だ。他方のVoxは、党首アバスカル氏とトランプ氏との関
係の近さから対米関係は重視するとみられる。一方で、Voxは国家主権を重視しEU懐疑主義的立場を
とることから、EUの政策が自国の産業や農業に影響を及ぼすことに強く反発しており、欧州グリーン
ディールに基づく規制の撤廃やEU・メルコスール間の貿易協定への反対を掲げている。 
防衛については、PP、Voxのいずれも投資増加を掲げている。ただし、PPはNATOにおける欧州の役
割強化に貢献する方針を示す一方で、Voxは自国の国境管理や治安維持など、自国の主権と領土の保
全を前面に打ち出している。

### Page 5

３．政権交代はスペインのみならず欧州全体の政治・経済にも影響を及ぼす可能性 
政権交代が実現すれば、スペインのみならず欧州全体の政治・経済に影響が及ぶことが予想される。 
近年のスペインの好景気の背景にあった寛容な移民政策は、最大の焦点となっている住宅問題への
対応を目的に厳格化されるだろう。図表6では、資本投入やTFP（全要素生産性）等の条件を一定にし
たうえで、移民流入がサンチェス政権下と同様のペースで続く場合（シナリオ①）、そのペースが半
減する場合（シナリオ②）、流入がゼロになる場合（シナリオ③）の三つのシナリオ別に2026～2030
年の平均的な潜在成長率を試算している。シナリオ①では現在と同程度の潜在成長率が維持される一
方、シナリオ②では①に比べて0.5％ポイント程度、シナリオ③では同1％ポイント弱低下する結果と
なった。PP、Voxのいずれも正規ルートで流入する労働力としての移民受け入れを否定しているわけ
ではなく、近年増加している高スキル人材の流入3は今後も続くとみられることには留意が必要だ。た
だし、住宅供給の増加には時間を要することを踏まえれば、移民流入数の抑制に政策の重点が置かれ
ることも想定される。スペインは近年の高成長により欧州経済を下支えしていたことから、今後は欧
州全体の成長率も減速する恐れがある。 
また、新政権では改善が続いていた財政に負荷がかかる懸念がある。スペインには、コロナ禍から
の経済復興を目的に創設されたEU復興基金（Next Generation EU）から1,000億ユーロ超（GDP比6.7％）
の巨額の資金が割り当てられている。これが自国の財政負担を抑えながら投資を拡大することを可能
にし、高成長と財政健全化の両立に寄与してきた。こうした状況を踏まえて主要格付け会社はスペイ
ン国債の格付けを相次いで引き上げたほか、同国の10年債利回りは政治・経済の停滞が続くフランス
を下回るなど、市場もスペインの経済・財政状況を好感している（図表10）。しかしながら、復興基
金の受け取りは今年末で終了する。こうした中でPPやVoxは各種減税やインフラ投資の実施を掲げて
いる。PPはEU財政ルールの順守を公約しているが、物価高や住宅問題を背景に歳出拡張圧力が強まる
中、財政規律と有権者の要求への対応を両立することは容易ではない。復興基金を用いた投資が細る
ことで成長率の減速が見込まれることもあり、今後財政が悪化に転じる恐れがある。 
PPとVoxが連立を組めば、少数与党であったサンチェス政権とは異なり、議会の過半数を握る公算

が大きい。もっとも、それだけで政治の安定を楽観視することはできない。両党には対EU政策や防衛、
移民政策等で調整を要する論点が多く、政策運営において両党の足並みが乱れる恐れがある4。カタル
ーニャやバスクの独立派・地域主義政党との間で、独立や自治権の在り方を巡る対立が強まるリスク
にも注意が必要だ。サンチェス政権は、少数与党であるが故に独立派政党の協力を必要とし、その見
返りとして恩赦などの譲歩を実施した。こうした姿勢は、独立派との関係改善や独立運動の沈静化に
寄与したと考えられる。一方、PPとVoxはともに国家の統一を重視し、自治州における中央政府の役
割強化や独立を目指す住民投票への厳格な対応を掲げており、独立派・地域主義政党との関係が悪化
する懸念がある。また、PPが憲法の枠内で自治州の自治を認める一方、Voxは国家の統一や領土の一
体性を脅かす政党の非合法化まで主張するなど、その姿勢には隔たりがある。独立問題が再び過熱す
る場合、独立派との対立のみならず、その対応を巡る連立内部の意見の相違も表面化し、政権基盤が
揺らぐ恐れがある。 
サンチェス氏は、NATOの防衛費目標への対応や、米国・イスラエルによるイラン攻撃への協力を
巡ってトランプ米大統領と激しく対立してきた。選挙後は、トランプ氏と関係が近いアバスカル氏率
いるVoxが政権入りすることが予想されるほか、PP、Voxのいずれも防衛費増額に前向きな姿勢を示し
ていることなどから、対米関係は改善する可能性がある。もっとも、防衛費に関しては、規模や使途
に不透明な点も残る。PP、Voxのいずれも防衛費の金額規模については明言していないほか、Voxに関
しては、移民対策や治安維持に防衛費を充当する方針を示しており、ロシアの脅威を念頭に置いたEU
やNATOの防衛力強化の方向性とは必ずしも一致しない。また、そもそもスペインはロシアからの距
離が遠く、地政学的リスクが相対的に低いことから、国民の間で防衛・安全保障政策を重視する意識
が低い（図表11）。NATOの目標達成に必要な予算を防衛費に振り向けられるかは、依然として不透明
図表10 10年債利回り 図表11 EU加盟国民の防衛・安全保障の優先度と

### Page 7

7

だ。 
EU政治への影響も小さくないだろう。このところ、EU域内では左派政権の数が減少している（図表
12）。こうした中で、主要国で唯一左派主導の政権を維持してきたスペインで右派への政権交代が実
現すれば、欧州理事会や欧州議会におけるパワーバランスが変化する要因となり得る。サンチェス氏
はこれまで、加盟国首脳が集まりEUの政策の大枠を定める欧州理事会において、中道左派の立場を反
映させる役割を担ってきた。同氏が退陣すれば、EUにおける中道左派の影響力が弱まり、移民政策や
通商政策、対中政策等が一層右傾化・保護主義化する可能性がある。影響は、欧州議会の会派間協力
に及ぶことも想定される。EUでは、欧州理事会の決定を踏まえて、EUの執行機関である欧州委員会が
法案を提出し、EU市民が直接選ぶ議員で構成される欧州議会と、加盟国の閣僚から成るEU理事会が共
同で審議する。サンチェス氏率いるPSOEは欧州議会の第二会派である中道左派の社会民主進歩同盟
（S&D）で最大の国別議員団を構成していることから、同党は欧州理事会における首脳間合意とS&D
をつなぐ政治的接点となってきた。PSOEが下野すれば、S&Dに属するスペイン議員団は首脳間の合意
に歩調を合わせて会派内の意見を取りまとめる動機が薄れることが予想される。その結果、フォン・
デア・ライエン欧州委員会を支える中道右派・中道左派・リベラル会派の協調が揺らぎ、重要法案や
次期中期予算を巡る合意形成が難しくなるリスクがある。また、EU懐疑主義政党であるVoxが政権入
りし、安全保障や移民、脱炭素等の分野で国家主権や国内産業の保護を重視する姿勢が強まれば、EU
内の合意形成が一段と難しくなることが想定される。 
以上のように、スペインでは近年高成長が続いてきたが、その裏で生じた住宅問題等に対する有権
者の不満が政治の不安定化を招き、結果として従来の成長モデルの矛盾と限界が浮き彫りとなった。
新政権は、こうした問題を解決し国民の不満を和らげる政策運営が求められる一方で、移民受け入れ
の厳格化など、有権者の要求に対応するための措置が成長の勢いを弱める可能性がある。また、PPと
Voxの政策路線や独立派・地域主義政党への対応の違いを踏まえれば、議会で過半数を握ることがそ
のまま政権運営の安定につながるとは限らない。
政治・経済の両面で新政権は難しい政策運営を迫
られそうだ。 
右派への政権交代が実現すれば、EU内の政治
的な重心が一段と右に移り、移民政策や財政運営
などを巡る加盟国間の議論にも影響が及ぶ可能
性がある。 
今回の選挙は、スペイン国内の政治情勢にとど
まらず、欧州全体の政策の方向性を考えるうえで
も重要な意味を持つだろう。

定な政治運営、今年に入って二度発生している多数の死傷者を伴う鉄道事故等を背景に支持を落としていた。しかしながら、選
挙戦終盤では、右派政権の成立阻止を目指す左派有権者の支持が PSOE に集まっていること等を受けて、同党の支持率はやや持
ち直している。なお、スペインは 1970 年代までフランコによる独裁政権が続いていたこと等を背景に、欧州の中でも極右への

立の維持が難しくなる可能性がある。実際、州政府レベルでは移民政策を巡る対立を背景に Vo x が PP との連立を離脱した事例
もある

実質ＧＤＰ成長率の推移(年度）(前年比）
(資料）内閣府経済社会総合研究所「四半期別ＧＤＰ速報」
(年度）
予測

＜実質成長率：2026年度 0.9％、2027年度 1.3％、2028年度 1.1％を予想＞

１. 2026年 7-9月期の実質 GDPは、民間消費の落ち込みを主因として前期比年率▲0.1％と 4
四半期ぶりのマイナス成長となった。 
２. 物価上昇ペースの加速に伴う実質購買力の低下を主因として民間消費が低迷することな
どから、2026年 10-12月期、2027年 1-3月期の実質 GDP成長率は 0％台前半から半ばに
とどまることが予想される。2026年度後半の日本経済は低空飛行が続く可能性が高い。 
３. 2027年度に入ると、飲食料品の消費税率引き下げに伴う物価上昇率の低下から家計の実
質購買力が改善し、民間消費が回復に向かうほか、原材料価格の低下に伴う企業収益の
改善を受けて設備投資の増勢ペースが加速することが見込まれる。 
４. 実質 GDP成長率は 2026年度が 0.9％、2027年度が 1.3％、2028年度が 1.1％と予想する。
消費税率引き下げによる実質 GDPへの影響は 2026年度が▲0.1％、2027年度が 0.3％と
試算する。 
５. 消費者物価上昇率（生鮮食品を除く総合）は輸入物価の上昇が食料、日用品など幅広い
品目に波及し、2026年度末にかけては 3％台まで加速するが、飲食料品の消費税率引き
下げを受けて、2027年度には 1％台まで鈍化するだろう。消費者物価上昇率（生鮮食品
を除く総合）は、2026年度が 2.4％、2027年度が 1.5％、2028年度が 2.0％と予想する。 
ニッセイ基礎研究所 2026-11-17

2026～2028年度経済見通し（26年 11月）

経済研究部 経済調査部長 斎藤  太郎

### Page 2

（中東情勢の影響） 
中東情勢の緊迫化を受けて一時大幅に減少した日本の原油輸入量は、代替調達の進展によって持
ち直し、7月以降は前年の水準を上回って推移している。 
財務省の「貿易統計」で原油輸入の動向を確認すると、2026年 3月は攻撃前にホルムズ海峡を通
過・出港していた原油が日本に到着したため、影響はまだ限定的であったが、4、5月には中東産を
中心に原油輸入量が前年比で 6割前後の大幅減少となった。その後、ホルムズ海峡を経由しない中
東産に加え、米国を中心とした代替調達が進んだことから、6月には減少幅が大きく縮小し、7月
以降は、原油輸入量が前年の水準を上回っている。2025年時点で中東産が約 7割を占めていたナフ
サについては、中東からの輸入量は減少が続いているものの、米国、ペルー、アルジェリアからの
輸入量が急増するなど代替調達が進展している。

＜原油＞ ＜ナフサ＞

### Page 3

原油輸入に占める中東の割合（数量ベース）は 2025年時点で 94.0％だったが、足もとでは 60％
台まで低下し、米国の割合が 2025年の 3.8％から 30％台まで上昇している。また、ナフサ輸入に占
める中東の割合は 2026年 5月に 10％まで低下した後、7月以降は 50％前後まで持ち直している。
ただし、2025年の 73.8％と比べれば依然として低い水準にある。

価格面では、ドバイ原油の市況価格は 3月に 1バレル 130ドル近く（月中平均）まで急上昇した
後、6、7月には 80ドル前後まで低下した。その
後、8月に 80ドル台後半、9、10月には 110ドル
程度まで急上昇したが、11月には 100ドルを割り
込んでいる。一方、通関（入着）ベースの原油価
格は、市況価格の変動が遅れて反映されるが、3
月の 1バレル 60ドル台から 6月に 117ドルまで急
上昇した後、7月も 115ドルと高止まりした。8
月には 102ドル、9月には 101ドルまで低下した
ものの、市況価格が 6、7月に大きく低下したに
もかかわらず、通関価格は 9月まで 100ドルを上
回る高水準が続いている。 
原油の輸入量が前年の水準を回復する中、輸入価格が大幅に上昇したため、原油の輸入金額は 6
月に前年比で増加に転じた後、高い伸びが続いており、貿易赤字の拡大要因となっている。 
もともと、通関ベースの原油価格は、指標価格に調整金、タンカー運賃、保険料などが上乗せさ
れているが、中東情勢緊迫後は調整金、タンカー運賃が大幅に上昇したこと、代替調達先の米国か
らの輸入価格が中東を上回っていることなどから、市況価格との差が大きく拡大している。代替調
達が進展するもとでは、原油の輸入価格が市況価格ほど低下せずに貿易収支の悪化につながること
が見込まれる。

（AI関連財が輸出を牽引、交易条件の悪化を緩和） 
グローバルな AI関連需要の拡大を背景に、日本の輸出も AI関連財が牽引役となっている。財
務省の「貿易統計」をもとに、AI関連財1とそれ以外の財に分けて輸出動向を確認すると、輸出数
量全体はそれほど強くないものの、AI関連財は 2026年入り後、前年比二桁の高い伸びが続いてい
る。2026年 7-9月期の輸出数量は、AI関連財が前年比 13.7％と大きく増加した一方、非 AI関連財
は同 1.2％の小幅な増加にとどまった。AI関連財が輸出数量全体を下支えする構図となっている。 
AI関連財は数量だけでなく、価格の上昇も顕著となっている。世界的な AI投資の拡大に伴い、
半導体や電子部品などの需給の引き締まりがその背景にあると考えられる。2026年 7-9月期の AI
関連財の輸出価格は前年比 24.3％と大幅に上昇し、輸出数量の増加と合わせて輸出金額は同 38.0％

1 AI関連財は、WTOが公表する AI関連財（AI-Enabling-Products）103品目。2025年の輸出全体に占める AI関連財の割
合は 17.1％。

AI関連財の輸出は数量、価格ともに高い伸び
輸出数量 輸出価格 輸出金額
（注）AI関連財はWTOが公表しているAI関連財（AI-Enabling Products）
（資料）財務省「貿易統計」
（前年比）
（年・四半期）
＜非AI関連財＞ ＜AI関連財＞

こうした AI関連財を中心とする輸出価格の上昇は、中東情勢の緊迫化に伴う交易条件の悪化を
緩和する役割も果たしている。原油をはじめとする資源価格の上昇は、輸入物価の上昇を通じて交
易条件を悪化させるが、今回は輸出物価も大幅に上昇している。このため、輸出物価を輸入物価で
除して求めた交易条件指数の悪化は比較的小幅にとどまっている。 
過去の資源価格高騰局面と比較すると、2005～2008年頃の世界的な資源価格高騰局面には、原
油などの輸入物価が大幅に上昇する一方、輸出物価の上昇がそれに追いつかず、交易条件が悪化し
た。また、2020年以降も、コロナ禍からの世界経済の回復に伴う資源価格の上昇に加え、2022年
のロシアによるウクライナ侵攻を受けた資源価格の急騰や円安の進行によって、交易条件が大幅に
悪化した。両局面ともに交易条件の累積的な悪化幅は▲30％以上となった。これに対し、2026年
1-3月期を起点とした 7-9月期までの 2四半期の悪化幅は▲6.6％である。AI関連財を中心とする輸
出価格の上昇が、原油価格の高騰に伴う輸入物価上昇の影響を一部相殺し、交易条件の悪化を緩和
している点が、過去の資源価格高騰局面との大き
な違いである。 
ただし、輸入物価の上昇率は輸出物価の上昇
率を上回っており、交易条件の悪化が続いている
ことに変わりはない。今後も原油価格が高止まり
する場合には、海外への所得流出を通じて国内経
済を下押しする可能性がある。一方、AI関連需
要の拡大を背景とした輸出価格の上昇が続けば、
交易条件の悪化を和らげる要因となるだろう。

（2027年の春闘賃上げ率は前年を若干上回る見込み） 
名目賃金を消費者物価（持家の帰属家賃を除く総合）で割り引いた実質賃金は、2026年 1月から
9ヵ月連続でプラスの伸びを維持している。名目賃金（現金給与総額）が 2025年中の 2％台前半か
ら 2026年入り後には 3％台へ伸びを高めたことに加え、政府によるエネルギー負担緩和策の影響な
どから、物価上昇率が大きく縮小したことが実質賃金の押し上げに寄与している。

消費者物価（持家の帰属家賃を除く総合）

2027年の春闘を取り巻く環境を確認すると、有効求人倍率が 1倍を上回り、失業率が 2％台半ば
で推移するなど、労働需給は引き締まった状態が続いており、企業収益は中東情勢緊迫化の影響を
受けながらも過去最高水準の更新を続けている。消費者物価は、政府によるエネルギー負担緩和策
の影響で 2026年入り後 1％台の伸びが続いていたが、9月に 9ヵ月ぶりの 2％台となり、今後上昇
ペースが加速することが確実となっている。 
賃上げの環境を過去と比較するために、労働需給（有効求人倍率）、企業収益（売上高経常利益
率）、物価（消費者物価上昇率（除く生鮮食品））について、過去平均（1985年～）からの乖離幅を
標準偏差で基準化してみると、3指標の合計は物価上昇率の鈍化を主因として前年を若干下回って
いるものの、企業収益のプラス幅が大きく拡大していることから、全体としては引き続き高水準と
なっている。賃上げの環境は引き続き良好と判断される。

2. 実質成長率は 2026年度 0.9％、2027年度 1.3％、2028年度 1.1％を予想

（2026年度後半は低成長が続く見通し） 
2026年 7-9月期のマイナス成長の主因となった民間消費の減少は、天候不順による一時的な影響
が大きい。ただし、こうした影響が解消されても、物価上昇の加速が消費の回復を妨げる可能性が
高い。消費者物価上昇率は 2026年 9月に 9ヵ月ぶりの 2％台となったが、原材料価格の上昇を耐久
財や日用品などの販売価格に転嫁する動きが今後さらに広がることから、2026年度末にかけて 3％
台まで高まる公算が大きい。物価上昇率の高まりによる実質購買力の低下を背景に 2026年度後半
の民間消費は弱めの動きが続くことが予想される。 
現時点では、2026年 10-12月期はプラス成長に復帰するものの、民間消費が伸び悩むことなどか
ら、前期比年率 0.6％とマイナス成長の後としては低めの伸びにとどまると予想している。2027年
1-3月期は、物価上昇率が 3％台まで高まることに加え、飲食料品の消費税率引き下げ前の買い控
えの影響もあり、民間消費は前期比でマイナスとなる可能性が高く、実質 GDP成長率は前期比年
率 0.2％とほぼゼロ成長にとどまるだろう。2026年度後半の日本経済は物価高による民間消費の低
迷を主因として、低空飛行が続くことが予想される。

予測

（消費税率引き下げの影響） 
政府は 2027年 4月から 2年間に限り、飲食料品（酒類、外食を除く）の消費税率を現在の 8％か
ら 1％に引き下げるとともに、1％相当の範囲内で中低所得者向けの給付を先行導入する予定として
いる。 
食料品の消費税率を 8％から 1％に引き下げると、家計の負担は年間 4.3兆円程度軽減される。こ
れにより期待されるのは、物価の低下、個人消費、実質 GDPの押し上げである。ニッセイ基礎研
究所のマクロモデルによる機械的な試算によれば、食料品の消費税率を 8％から 1％に引き下げた
場合、消費者物価（総合）は▲1.4％低下し、個人消費は 0.7％、実質 GDPは 0.5％押し上げられる。 
ただし、これは消費税率の引き下げ分（7％）が全て値下げに反映された場合の試算である。こ
れまで日本では消費税率引き上げ時にほぼ 100％価格転嫁されていたが、引き下げ時にそれが当て

（家計の実質可処分所得は依然としてコロナ禍前を下回る） 
2026年度入り後の家計消費の低迷は、高校授業料や小学校給食費の無償化による政府消費への振
り替え、豪雨・長雨などの天候不順の影響
もあるが、より長い目でみれば、実質可処
分所得の伸び悩みが消費低迷の主因である。
家計の実質可処分所得（季節調整値）はこ
のところ持ち直しているが、依然としてコ
ロナ禍前（2019年平均）の水準を若干下回
っている。 
実質家計可処分所得の内訳をみると、雇用
者報酬は物価高による実質ベースの低下が
続いていたが、名目賃金上昇率の高まりと

（恒常的な所得に連動する家計消費） 
実質家計消費と実質可処分所得は、平常時には連動性が非常に高い。しかし、コロナ禍で一人当
たり 10万円の特別定額給付金が支給された際は、厳しい行動制限が課せられていたことから貯蓄
率が急上昇し、両者が大きく乖離した。また、2024年に所得税・住民税減税が行われた際も可処分
所得の増加は消費の押し上げにほとんど寄与しなかった。 
このことは、消費の持続的な回復を実現するためには、一時的な所得の増加ではなく、恒常的な
所得の増加が重要であることを示している。 
実質可処分所得は、名目賃金の高い伸びが続く一方、物価の上昇ペースが加速することから、2026
年度末にかけて低迷し、消費も停滞することが予想される。2027年度に入ると、食料品の消費税率
引き下げに伴う物価上昇率の低下によって実質
可処分所得が押し上げられ、消費も伸びを高め
るだろう。ただし、消費税率の引き下げが 2年
間の時限措置となっているため、実質所得の増
加のかなりの部分は貯蓄に回ることが想定され
る。このため、実質可処分所得の増加に比べて
家計消費の増加は小幅にとどまる公算が大きい。
家計貯蓄率は足もとの 1％程度から 2027年度に
は 2％台まで上昇するだろう。 
民間消費は 2026年度が前年比 0.0％、2027年
度が同 1.4％、2028年度が同 0.9％と予想する。

（設備投資は回復するが、供給制約が抑制要因に） 
2026年 7-9月期の設備投資は前期比 1.6％の高い伸びとなったが、4-6月期に特許権の海外譲渡に
伴う研究開発投資の減少によって落ち込んだ反動によって押し上げられた面がある。設備投資は回
復しているものの、企業収益が過去最高の更新を続けていることを考慮すれば、そのペースは緩や
かなものにとどまっている。 
日銀短観などの設備投資計画の強さに対し、GDP統計の設備投資が弱く見える理由のひとつは、
設備投資計画は名目（金額）で、GDP統計の設備投資は設備投資デフレーターで割り引いた実質で
みることが多いことである。名目設備投資は 2020年 4-6月期の 94.1兆円（季節調整済・年率換算
値）を底に着実に増加し、2026年 7-9月期には 135.4兆円となり、この間の伸びは 43.8％となって

### Page 10

また、企業の設備投資意欲は強いものの、人手不足などの供給制約により工事の進捗が遅れてい
ることも設備投資の抑制要因となっている可能性がある。機械受注残高と建設工事の手持ち工事高
を、それぞれ販売額、施工高の何ヵ月分に相当するかでみると、いずれも長期的に増加傾向にある。
高水準の受注残高は先行きの設備投資拡大を示唆すると同時に、人手不足などの供給制約により機
械の生産や建設工事が進まず、計画に比べて実際の設備投資の進捗が遅れている可能性も示してい
る。

設備投資は、高水準の企業収益を背景に、人手不足対応の省力化投資、デジタル化に向けた情報
関連投資、AI・半導体関連等の成長分野への投資、Eコマース拡大に伴う建設投資などの案件を軸
に、先行きも増加傾向が続くことが予想される。ただし、資材価格の高騰や人手不足による工事の
遅れから、企業収益の高い伸びや設備投資意欲の強さに比べればそのペースは緩やかにとどまる可
能性が高い。 
設備投資は 2025年度の前年比 2.2％から 2026年度に同 1.0％と減速した後、2027年度が同 2.8％、
2028年度が同 2.7％と伸びを高めると予想する。

（物価の見通し） 
消費者物価（生鮮食品を除く総合、以下コア CPI）は、2026年 1月に前年比 1.9％と 2022年 3月
以来、3年 10ヵ月ぶりの 2％割れとなった後、1％
台の伸びが続いていたが、9月には同 2.1％と 9
ヵ月ぶりの 2％台となった。 
コア CPI上昇率が 1％台で推移していたのは、
主として補助金政策によって物価上昇率が押し
下げられているためである。ガソリンや電気・
ガス代等の負担緩和策、教育無償化政策などの
特殊要因は 2025年 12月以降、物価上昇率の押
し下げ要因となっており、2026年 9月の押し下

消費者物価（生鮮食品を除く総合）の推移
差（公表値－特殊要因を除く試算値）
（注）特殊要因は①消費税率の変更、②教育無償化政策（2026年4月の学校給食無償化政策を含む）、
③2021年の携帯電話通信料の引き下げ、④旅行支援策、⑤ガソリンや電気・ガス代等の負担緩和策

公表値
特殊要因を除く
（資料）総務省統計局「消費者物価指数」、日本銀行「消費者物価のコア指標」
（年・月）

### Page 11

食料（生鮮食品を除く）は 2025年 7月の前年比 8.3％をピークに鈍化傾向が続き、2026年 8月に
同 2.7％となった後、9月には同 2.9％と 14ヵ月ぶりに上昇率が拡大した。帝国データバンクの「食
品主要 195社価格動向調査」（10/30公表）によれば、2026年（1～12月）の飲食料品の値上げ品目
数は 2万 327品目となり、前年同時期の 2万
580品目を若干下回っている。中東情勢悪化
に伴う原材料価格の高騰を受けて、先行き 3
ヵ月の値上げ品目数は 2026年 6月以降、前年
比で増加に転じているが、そのペースはやや
鈍化している。消費者物価の食料（生鮮食品
を除く）は、上昇率が高まるものの、米価格
の下落が当面続くこともあり、2025年夏場に
比べればそのペースは緩やかにとどまること
が見込まれる。 
一方、エネルギー価格は、政府による負担緩和策によって抑制されてきたが、電気・ガス料金へ
の支援策は 2026年 9月使用分（消費者物価への反映は 10月）でいったん終了した。2026年 12月
使用分からは支援策が再開されるものの、補助金額が前年を若干下回ることに加え、2026年春以降
の原油価格高騰の影響が燃料費調整単価に遅れて反映されることから、今冬の電気・ガス料金は前
年を大きく上回ることが見込まれる。さらに、政府は、ガソリン価格の高騰を抑制するため、2026
年 3月から 11月まで、補助金によってレギュラーガソリンの全国平均小売価格を 1リットル当た
り 170円程度に抑えてきたが、12月以降は補助金を段階的に縮小し、価格抑制の基準価格を毎月 5
円ずつ引き上げる方針を示した。このため、エネルギー価格は 2026年 9月に前年比▲0.6％から上
昇ペースが急加速し、2027年入り後には前年比で二桁の伸びとなり、コア CPI上昇率への寄与度は
1％を超えるだろう。 
今回の見通しでは、電気・ガス料金への支
援策については、2027年夏以降も補助金を段
階的に縮小しながら継続し、ガソリン補助金
については、価格抑制の基準価格を 2027年 2
月に 1リットル当たり 185円まで段階的に引
き上げた後、同水準で据え置くことを前提と
している。

Key Takeaways
We continue to see margin improvement, with October NPM at 23.4%, above
20.1% in 3Q26.
Margin improvement likely driven by combination of better pricing, improving
mix, and higher utilization for ABF and BT substrates.
Stay OW on both Unimicron and NYPCB – but on a 12-month view, we prefer
Unimicron (12.4x CY27e P/E) over NYPCB (13.0 CY27e P/E).

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Unimicron (3037.TW)
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate
value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.2%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 14%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF substrate demand from PC and server customers

n Capex cuts; halt to its capacity expansion plan
n Continued yield issues of alternative technology that doesn't require substrate (e.g.,
CoWoP)
Risks to Downside
n Sudden demand shortfall
n Technological change that would not require ABF substrates
n Intensifying competition
n Yield issues or production hiccups when ramping new capacity
Nan Ya PCB (8046.TW)
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate

value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.3%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 17%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF and BT demand
n Faster-than-expected pickup in AI and 5G demand
n Stronger-than-expected ASP hike
n Continued yield issues of alternative technology that doesn't require substrate (e.g.

CoWoP)
Risks to Downside
n Sudden demand shortfall, creating headwinds for ABF substrate demand and pricing
n Technological change that would not require ABF substrates
n Intensifying competition

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

台新證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市仁愛路四段 118 號 16 樓(02)5589-9558  (113)金管投顧新字第 021 號 
公司拜訪快報

雙鴻(3324)：3Q26 財報不如預期，營運逐季向上 
建議：維持買進

一、雙鴻為散熱解決方案供應商  雙鴻成立於 1998 年 8 月，為國內前二大 NB 散熱模組廠，過去主要生產散熱
器、散熱片、散熱模組、散熱導管、風扇等，現在已轉型為提供客戶完整散熱解
決方案供應商。主要應用於筆記型電腦、桌上型電腦、智慧型手機及伺服器等領
域。3Q26 產品應用領域 Server 81%、PC 14%、VGA/Gaming 4%、及 Others(手
機及車用) 1%。另外 3Q26 水冷業務比重 62%(1H26 約為 55~60%)。

二、3Q26 毛利率 25.65%，低於
預期，3Q26 EPS 11.59 元 
 雙鴻 3Q26 EPS 11.59 元，低於台新預估的 14.13 元，獲利達成率 82%。本業
來看，3Q26 營收 97.87 億元，+12.5%QOQ，+64.3%YOY，低於市場預期之
+15~20%QOQ，七月營收有遞延認列，實際拉貨力道未如預期顯著，3Q26 毛
利率 25.65%，較 2Q26 再衰退 2.77 個百分點，亦低於台新預估的 30%，主要
是報廢品打消，預期 4Q26 毛利率回升，3Q26 EPS 11.59 元。雙鴻 10 月營收
33.72 億元，+17%MOM，+45%YOY，獲利達成率 31%，符合預期，預期 11 月
營收及 12 月營收將優於 10 月，預估 4Q26 營收 110.37 億元，+12.8%QOQ，
+45.6%YOY，預估 4Q26 毛利率 28%，預估 4Q26 EPS 14.79 元。

三、營運逐季成長，氣冷及水冷
兩塊業務均樂觀 
 2025 年雙鴻水冷營收比重 35%，1Q26 提升至 55%，3Q26 提升至 62%，預期
2027 年將提升至 80%，帶動公司業績大幅增長。氣冷部分，2024 年月營收 2-
2.5 億元，2025 年 4～4.5 億元，2026 年平均 6～7 億元，氣冷水冷均樂觀，以
AWS TR3 來看，氣冷及水冷比重至少是 70~80%對比 20~30%，TR4 將為 100%
全水冷，水冷大趨勢不變，VR 9 月起量，VR 營收逐季增，TR3 9 月起量 Compute 
tray 及 Switch tray 及 Rack manifolds 都有相關產品出貨，預計 12 月單月 ASIC
營收比重有機會來到 15~25%，ASIC 水冷產值高於GPU 客戶，其中 Mata>AWS，
2027 年 ASIC 增長可期，公司預期 2027 年 ASIC 營收將超過 GPU。2026 年可
視為 CDU 元年，2027 年營收跳增可期。台新預估 4Q26 營收 110.37 億元，

Document 196: 6515穎崴 亞東

Source PDF: all report pdf/6515穎崴 亞東.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-19

### Page 1

◼ 投資建議: 
美系 AI GPU 與客製化 ASIC 持續開案，帶動高階測試座需求。模型預估，穎崴的 NVIDIA 
系統級測試座（SLT）營收將由 2026 年的 33.26 億元增至 2027 年的 83.45 億元，貢獻公
司逾半營收增量。公司目標於 2027 上半年將自製彈簧針月產能提升至 1,400 萬針，有助降
低外購成本，支撐全年毛利率由 40.92% 升至 42.97%。穎崴的矽光子與 CPO 測試方案已涵蓋
晶圓、封裝及模組，若客戶專案進入量產，可望帶來額外營收。 
根據模型，穎崴(6515)目標價 5,711 元。依模型預估的 2027 年 EPS 163.18 元 × 基準本益比 
35 倍估算，較 2026/11/18 收盤價 3,560 元具 60.4% 潛在上漲空間。建議買進 
◼ 未來觀察： 
1. 探針自製率&產能狀況 
2. HPC/AI 業務成長性 
◼ 公司是否涉及經營權爭奪之情事：無。 
◼ 產業概況： 
1. 半導體測試介面市場:系統測試需求高速成長。 
AI GPU、伺服器 CPU 與雲端業者自研 ASIC 擴張，帶動高階晶片封裝後最終測試（FT）與
系統級測試（SLT）需求成長。FT 把關量產電性品質；SLT 模擬實際系統與軟體運作，補足 
FT 對系統整合、長時間與極端負載之測試不足，隨晶片複雜度提升，滲透率持續走高。 
市場預估全球高階 HPC FT/SLT Socket TAM 將自 2026E 9 億美元躍升至 2028E 37 億美元，
CAGR 約 104%（SLT 達約 110%）。動能來自時間與耗材乘數效應：2025–2028E AI 晶片 
FT/SLT 測試時間 CAGR 達 15–20%（如 Rubin SLT 增至 120–150+ 分鐘），壓低單槽產出
並迫使客戶擴增平行測試槽位。隨大封裝、萬級以上高 Pin 數、高頻高速及破千瓦功耗成為
主流，高階測試座須整合訊號完整性、大電流與溫控散熱，帶動 FT Socket 單顆價值量提升 2 
至 3 倍；長時高功耗壓接亦加速探針磨損與更換，創造 4 至 5 倍耗材數量乘數效應。

### Page 2

◼ 公司簡介： 
1. 穎崴主要專注於半導體測試介面之研發設計與製造銷售，目前作為全球最大測試座
（Test Socket）供應商。主要產品涵蓋後端 FT Test、SLT Test、與 Burn-in Test 用之同軸
測試座（Coaxial Socket）、 HyperSocket、Burn-in Socket。今年與 TPI 簽訂五年合作契約
積極擴展前端晶圓測試 CP Test 用之 MEMS Probe Card，並整合主動溫控與液冷技術開
發高工耗測試逤需要的散熱測試解決方案。穎崴目前主要生產基地分布於高雄，分別為
高雄探針廠、高雄仁武廠，仁武廠包含一廠與二廠，其中仁武二廠已於 2Q27 投產。新
竹廠負責探針卡製造。 
2. 1H26 營收結構為 Coaxial Socket 48%、Probe Card 27%、PoP & Plastic Socket 14%、
Contact Element 6%、Others 3%、Burn-in Socket 2%。若按應用別區分，HPC & AI 占比
已達 70%居應用類別首位，其次 Smart Phone 8%、而 Networking／車用及其他合計 
22%。至 3Q26，依 Citi 調研，隨探針卡專案出貨階段性收尾及高階測試座放量，FT 
Test、SLT Test & Burn-in Socket 合計比重回升至 72%。其中 Coaxial Socket 預估 60%、
RF & Plastic Socket 大概 11%、Burn-in Socket 則佔 1%。至於 Probe Card 預估降至 20% 
3. 公司整體合併毛利率於 1Q26 為 43.0%、2Q26 因 MEMS 探針卡占比拉升及新產能折舊影
響降至 38.3%，3Q26 隨測試座比重提高毛利率已回升至 40.5%。各產品線方面，依摩
根大通調研，Socket 與 Contact Elements 毛利率穩定維持在 40%+。而 MEMS Probe Card 
因須外購關鍵探針元件，因此即便 2026 年生效之二期協議已改善獲利，毛利率仍僅約 
30–35%。目前穎崴 Pogo pin 自製程度高達 60%，隨自製彈簧針 pogo pin 月產能由 1H26
的 600 萬 pins 擴至 1H27 的 1,400 萬 pins，將有助進一步支撐 Socket 毛利率。 
4. 客戶比重:截至 2026 年活躍客戶超過 200 家，但營收高度集中於高階 AI、HPC、GPU、
ASIC 與伺服器 CPU 客戶。前十大客戶合計占營收比重達 90%，且 7 奈米及以下先進

### Page 3

製程產品占比高達 89%。根據摩根大通，2026 全年穎崴核心終端客戶營收占比以
Nvidia 居首，佔比大約 50–60%。其次為 AMD，預計 10–15%與 Google 約 2%。
◼ 成長動能：
1. AI GPU／ASIC 推升 SLT 與高階 FT 測試座需求)
AI 晶片朝大封裝、高 Pin 數、高功耗與高速傳輸升級，拉長測試時間並放大 SLT Test 導入
需求，使整體市場進入倍數擴張期。在 TAM 動能上，全球高階 HPC FT Test／SLT Test 測試
座市場規模預計自2026E 的 9 億美元躍升至2028E 的 37 億美元，市場預估從 2026 到 2028
相關產品的市場CAGR 將逾 100%，其中價值量更高的 SLT Test 測試座將由 7 億美元跳升至
31 億美元。隨穎崴切入 Nvidia SLT Test 供應鏈，目前市占率預估由 2026E 的 20% 升至
2028E 的30%，外加 2027 年雲端業者如 Google、AMD 的自研 ASIC 與伺服器 CPU 擴大量產，

FT Test／SLT Test 測試座占穎崴總營收比重預估將於 2027E–2028E 維持於 64%–79% 區
間。根據自有模型估算，測試座業務於 2027E 預計貢獻營收約新台幣 161 億–192 億元、
2028E 擴增至約 274 億–362 億元，整體測試座 2026–2028E 營收 CAGR 區間約為 61%–
89%。
2. HyperSocket 與液冷熱管理產品放量
下一代 AI 晶片正由約 10000 Pin 邁向20000 Pin 以上，且由於 AMD MI455、TPUv9 系列新
世代平台封裝尺寸將跨過 100×100mm 至 120×120mm 門檻，基板翹曲與大電流散熱成為測試瓶
頸。穎崴專利 HyperSocket 結合主動液冷熱管理方案可克服大尺寸封裝翹曲並維持穩定電
性，因此Pogo Pin 單針 ASP 相較既有高階同軸測試座享有約 30%溢價，帶動產品組合持續升
級，目前穎崴公司預估 HyperSocket 與液冷測試解決方案將於 2027 占總營收 5%，但考量客

戶大封裝世代切換節奏，普遍預期放量重心將落在 2028 年。依自有模型的預估，該測試解決
方案預計於2027 貢獻總營收約 3%–5%（約新台幣 9 億–15 億元），2028 有機會放大至約
5%–6%（約新台幣 21 億–32 億元），相較 2027 年呈現倍數級年增動能，並有助高階測試座
在擴產自製化下將 2027E–2028E 合併毛利率支撐於 41.1%–46.2% 區間。

### Page 4

3. 新增產能釋放與 CPO 測試
因應既有產能滿載，高雄仁武二廠已於2Q27 投產，其中新增 Socket 產能自 3 月開出並在第
二季順利認列營收，使 5,000-pin 的等效規格 Socket 月產能自 2026 下半年的 4.2k 套擴增逾
70%至 1H27 的 7.2k 套，自製 Pogo Pin 月產能亦由 900 萬 pins 增逾 50%至 1,400 萬 pins，預
估 2027 年底可達單月 1,600–1,800 萬 pins。新產能開出讓既有高階 AI 積壓的訂單順利轉
化為營收，支撐目前市場與自有模型所預估的穎崴 2027E 總營收區間達新台幣 213.5 億–
303.7 億元（YoY +45%–85%）、2028E 達 351.0 億–528.3 億元（YoY +64%–76%），對應
2026–2028E 總營收 CAGR 區間為 54%–81%（EPS CAGR 區間則達 76%–108%）。CPO 的測試
方案預計2H27 開始小量出貨，營收貢獻大約低個位數以下，至 2028 年進入量產成熟階段，

預計可以貢獻2028 年營收最高至接近 10%。
4. 1H26
1H26 合併營收 65.03 億元，HoH +61.0%、YoY +70.3%（1Q26 為 29.80 億元、2Q26 為
35.23 億元），整體毛利率為 40.5%（1Q26 為 43.0%、2Q26 為 38.3%）。稅後淨利 13.71 億
元（YoY +67.6%、HoH +60.4%；1Q26 為 6.99 億元、2Q26 為 6.72 億元），1H26 累計 EPS
達 38.24 元（1Q26 為 19.54 元、2Q26 為 18.70 元），上半年營收與獲利均創歷年同期新
高。截至最新已公告進度，累計前 9 月合併營收達 110.59 億元（年增 96.7%），同創歷年
同期新高。在產品組合與毛利率比較上，1H26 產品營收比重仍以 Coaxial Socket（48%）與
Probe Card（27%）為核心。
⚫ 與1H25（毛利率 48.9%）比較：1H26 毛利率表現明顯較差（YoY -8.4 個百分點），主

因1H25 銷售較多高毛利的 Non-MEMS Probe Card 及傳統高毛利組合，而 1H26 向
Technoprobe 外購關鍵 MEMS 探針元件的 MEMS Probe Card 營收占比擴大，上述外購元
件結構拉低 MEMS Probe Card 毛利率，使其落在 30%–35%，低於測試座及接觸元件的
40% 以上水準，對整體毛利率形成結構性稀釋。

### Page 5

⚫ 2H26E 穎崴合併營收 79.56 億元（HoH +22.3%、YoY +97.0%），毛利率 41.29%（HoH 
+0.82 ppt、YoY -0.58 ppt），歸屬母公司稅後淨利 17.71 億元（HoH +29.2%、YoY 
+107.1%），基本 EPS 48.76 元（HoH +27.5%、YoY +103.5%）。

⚫ 2026E 穎崴合併營收 144.60 億元（YoY +84.0%），毛利率 40.92%（YoY -4.34 
ppt），歸屬母公司稅後淨利 31.42 億元（YoY +87.8%），基本 EPS 87.04 元（YoY 
+85.5%）。 
5. 2026/2027 
產能與資本支出方面，仁武二廠與下一座新廠預計於 2Q27–1Q28 陸續投產，推升 2027 年探
針月產能達 1,400 萬–1,800 萬支、自製率提高至 50%–60%，5,000-pin 等效測試座月產能於 
1H27 達 7.2k 套等於穎崴整體測試座產能將在 2027 倍增，2027–2028 年度預估的資本支出介
於新台幣 20.0 億–24.0 億元，其中自有模型預估 2027 為 20.0 億到 22.0 億元之間、2028 預估
為 22.0 億到 24.0 億元之間。新產品線方面，受惠高階 Functional Burn-in 升級，2027–2028E 
Burn-in 測試座營收預估為新台幣 6.40 億–10.53 億元，2027 模型預估約 6.40 億元、2028 則
有望到 10.53 億元，占總營收約 3%成為未來關注的成長動能之一。CPO 與矽光子測試預計自 
2H27 起小量出貨，2028E 營收貢獻區間為 5%-10%不等的年營收，端看實際量產與交貨情況。。

⚫ 2027E 穎崴合併營收 238.42 億元（YoY +64.9%），毛利率 42.97%（YoY +2.05 ppt），
歸屬母公司稅後淨利 59.53 億元（YoY +89.5%），基本 EPS 163.18 元（ YoY +87.5%）。

⚫ 2028E 穎崴合併營收 407.33 億元（YoY +70.8%），毛利率 44.33%（YoY +1.36 ppt），
歸屬母公司稅後淨利 111.08 億元（YoY +86.6%），基本 EPS 304.52 元（ YoY +86.6%）。

3Q26 Review: “조삼모사” Again  
3Q26P 연결 실적은 매출액 6조 943억원(YoY -6.0%), 영업이익 4,520억원(YoY -46.2%, OPM 
7.4%)을 기록하며 컨센서스 대비 연결 매출액(7.5조원)과 영업이익(1.16조원) 모두 대폭 하회했다. 주
요 자회사가 양호한 실적을 기록했음에도 불구하고, 지상방산 실적이 매우 부진했다. 지상방산은 매출
액 1조 2,455억원(YoY -41.0%), 영업이익 217억원(YoY -96.2%, OPM 1.7%)을 기록했는데, 1)3Q 
생산 물량이 4Q 매출 인식되는 흐름 속, 고정비 부담이 컸었고, 2)낮은 수출 비중(3Q 48%, 2Q 58%)
과 국내외 사업의 부진한 믹스(국내 BEP 수준, 해외 OPM 11% 추정), 3)비우호적인 원/달러 환율, 4)
대전 사업장 사고 복구 및 기타 일회성 비용(300억원+160억원, 총 460억원) 등이 반영되었다.   
대전 사업장 정상화 시점과 사업 차질 영향은? 
대전 사업장은 내년 상반기 중 정상화될 예정이며, 보은과 여수 공장에서 생산 보완을 위한 조치가 진
행 중인 것으로 소통했다. 또한 대전 사업장은 국내 물량 위주이며, 수출 납기(천무, 천궁-II 추진체 
등)에는 지장이 없을 것이라고 언급했지만, 기타 사업장 램프업을 위한 인건비 증가 등, 수익성 악화는 
불가피할 것으로 예상된다.  
15%대의 매출 성장 가이던스는 달성이 가능할까? 
보수적으로 접근하는 것이 합리적이라는 판단이다. 당사는 ‘26E 지상방산 매출액을 일부 ‘27~28E로 
이연하면서 -6.1% 하향 조정했다. 사측은 폴란드 EC1 천무 인도 가이던스를 기존 40문에서 60문으
로 상향 조정하는 등 납품 속도가 예정대비 빨라진 것을 언급했다. 다만, 천무 EC1에서 미사일 물량 
비중이 높은 것으로 파악되기 때문에 생산 지연 가능성과 수출입 데이터 상 12월 출하 물량의 매출 인
식 이월 가능성을 반영했다. ‘27E는 폴란드 후속 사업과 루마니아 K9, 호주 레드백 등이 양산 페이즈
에 진입하며 성장세가 지속될 전망이다.    
투자의견 Buy, 목표주가 1,480,000원 유지 
실적 쇼크와는 별개로, 미국 K9MH 시제, 스페인 K9, 크로아티아 천무 등 수주는 서프라이즈라고 판
단된다. 분기말 수주잔고는 35조원(수출 비중 74%, 스페인 미포함)으로 중장기 성장성에 대한 이견은 
없다. 불가피한 분기 실적 변동으로 동사의 미래 가치를 예단하는 것은 시기상조라는 판단이다.

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 실적&임단협 우려 해소, 발전엔진 수요 폭발적HD현대중공업(329180) 실적&임단협 우려 해소, 발전엔진 수요 폭발_5484f7f5.pdf

### Page 1

HD 현대중공업 (329180/KS) 
실적&임단협 우려 해소, 발전엔진 수요 폭발적 
2026-10-29

조선/방산. 한승한, R.A 고서영 / shane.han@sks.co.kr / 3773-9992

매수(유지) 
목표주가: 640,000 원(하향) 
현재주가: 285,500 원 
상승여력: 124.2%

Signal: 3Q26, 조업일수 감소 및 환율 하락으로 시장 예상치 하회 
Key: 4Q26~1Q27 부터 본격적인 발전엔진 및 FDC 수주 모멘텀 전망 
Step: 상선 우려 감안해도 엔진&FDC 으로 밸류 매력도 충분. 매수 기회

3Q26 Review: 시장예상치 하회하는 실적 시현  
3Q26 연결 매출액 5조 7,536억원(+30.2% YoY, -9.1% QoQ), 영업이익 8,992억원
(+61.3% YoY, -13.5% QoQ)으로, 시장 예상치(9,792억원)를 하회했다. 조업일수 감소
(휴가 10 일+중대재해 4일) 및 환율 하락 영향(450 억원)과 더불어, 임단협 관련 예상 비
용 중 75%를 이번 분기 선반영 했기 때문이다. 상선 수주 빈티지는 중형 선박 기준 ‘23 년 
9.6%, ‘24년 65%, ‘25년 25%, 대형 선박 기준 ‘22년 4%, ‘23년 45%, ‘24년 31%, 
‘25년 20%로, 선가 상승효과 지속되는 중이다. 특히 해양 부문에서 루야 프로젝트의 공정
률 확대 효과 및 C/O로 180억원의 환입 발생으로 전 분기 대비 큰 폭의 이익 성장 시현했
다. 최근 키윗과 미국 내 합작 조선소 설립을 추진하며, 미 군수지원함 초도 2척 수주 및 향
후 물량 건조, 그리고 HII와의 함정 선체블록 하도급 사업 수주까지 기대할 수 있다.

투자의견 ‘매수’ 유지, 목표주가 64 만원으로 하향 
HD 현대중공업에 대한 투자의견 ‘매수’를 유지하고, 목표주가는 기존 82 만원에서 64 만원
으로 하향한다. 최근 상선 실적 피크아웃 우려에 따른 조선주 주가 급락으로 멀티플 하향 조
정했다. 하지만 상선 부문의 우려를 감안하더라도 상선 사이클과 무관한 육상 발전 엔진 및 
FDC 에 대한 글로벌 하이퍼스케일러들의 수요 확대는 지속되고 있는 중이다. 올해 공시 기
준으로 육상발전 엔진 약 1.6GW 수주한 것으로 알려졌으나, 미공시된 약 1.4GW 까지 포
함 시 총 3.0GW 이상을 수주한 상황이다. 캐파 증설 기준으로 ‘28 년 1.5GW, ‘29 년 
3GW 캐파에 해당하는 물량을 이미 대부분 채웠으며, 기존 계획 대비 빠른 준공 혹은 공정
률 확대, 더 나아가 추가적인 캐파 증설까지 고려할 수준으로 파악된다. 빠르면 올해 연말 
혹은 내년 초부터 4 행정 중속엔진&FDC 본격 수주를 통해 중장기적 신성장 동력 확보할 
전망이다. 현 주가 레벨에서 상승여력 충분히 남아있다는 판단으로, 3Q 실적 및 임단협 불
확성 해소된 현 시점에서 적극적 매수 의견 및 조선주 최선호주(Top-Pick)로 제시한다.

COMPLIANCE NOTICE 
작성자(한승한)는 본 조사분석자료에 게재된 내용들이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 신의성실하게 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 보고서에 언급된 종목의 경우 당사 조사분석담당자는 본인의 담당종목을 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
본 보고서는 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공된 사실이 없습니다. 
당사는 자료공표일 현재 해당기업과 관련하여 특별한 이해 관계가 없습니다. 
종목별 투자의견은 다음과 같습니다. 
투자판단 3 단계(6 개월기준) 15%이상 → 매수 / -15%~15% → 중립 / -15%미만 → 매도

투자의견 BUY  
매수, 유지 
6개월 
목표주가 540,000 
하향  
현재주가 
(26.10.28) 
285,500 
조선업종

◼ 글로벌 피어 평균 멀티플 하락을 반영하여 목표주가 하향 
◼ 3Q에 산재한 일회성 요인. 원화강세, 조업일수 감소, 임단협 비용 반영 
◼ 모두의 우려에도 불구하고 본업은 안정적으로 천천히 잘 진행되는 중

투자의견 매수, 목표주가 540,000원으로 -32.5% 하향 
목표주가는 28F EPS 42,343원에 글로벌 조선사 피어 평균 12.8배를 적용해 
산출함. 목표주가 하향은 글로벌 경기 불확실성과 중국 조선소 생산캐파 확장
으로 인한 공급과잉 리스크로 전반적인 산업 멀티플이 하락한 것을 반영함. 
다만 가스선 수주 모멘텀과 발전용 엔진의 수주 및 생산 확대 등에 따른 중장
기 성장 유효

3분기는 원래 비수기입니다 
동사의 2026년 3분기 매출액은 5조 7,536억원(+30.2% YoY, -9.1% QoQ), 영
업이익은 8,992억원(+61.3% YoY, -13.5% QoQ), OPM 15.6%를 기록. 3분기는 
1)원달러 환율 하락(전분기 대비 평균 -79원) 2)연휴로 인한 조업일수 감소(47
일), 3)임단협 관련 추가 발생 가능 비용 75% 반영)의 영향이 가장 컸음

중장기적인 모멘텀은 여전히 살아있다 
[상선] 중국 공급 확대에 따른 선가 및 수주 경쟁 심화는 부담이나 LNG선 발
주 재개 및 노후선 교체 수요는 긍정적. 동사의 고부가가치선 위주의 수주 전
략 방향은 유지될 예정 [해양] 루야 프로젝트 진행률 인식으로 4분기도 양호
한 이익률 기대, 다만 현재 수주 부진으로 블록, 혹은 LNG선 투입을 통해 매
출 하락을 방어하는 방안도 구상 중 [방산] 키윗과의 미국 합작 조선소를 통해 
콘솔탱커, 로로선 등 지원함 건조를 시작으로 전투함까지 확장 준비 [엔진] 국
가전력망 및 데이터센터향 발전 수요 확대, 하이퍼스케일러 직접협의로 성장
성 강화, 수주 확정 및 추진 물량 기준 28년 1.5GW, 29년 3GW 생산 계획 중

Document 273: An RBA peak of greater conviction (2026-11-25)

Source PDF: all report pdf/An RBA peak of greater conviction (2026-11-25).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-25
Pages: 10
Status: ok

### Page 1

#australia #luciellis #rba
An RBA peak of greater conviction
By Luci Ellis
Chief Economist Westpac Group, Westpac
08:56 November 13 2026 Share
We already believed the RBA cash rate was likely at a peak, even if RBA officials were less
convinced. A deeper housing downturn and early signs of cracks in the consumer sector
point to an even softer 2027. Fading inflation risks from oil also boost our conviction on
rates being at a peak.

### Page 2

It was already our view that the RBA was likely done hiking the cash rate after the November
increase. Since then, our conviction on that call has strengthened. Later today we will publish
the November Market Outlook with revised forecasts. GDP growth, the labour market and housing
market have all been revised down in 2027. Underlying inflation has been revised down slightly in the
second half of 2027, bringing the year-ended figure for calendar 2027 more decisively inside the

RBA’s 2–3% target range than our previous forecast round. The unemployment rate forecast has
been revised up to peak above 5%. Based on this revised forecast profile, an increase in the cash
rate from here would be unlikely and – we would argue – undesirable.
A range of considerations underlie this forecast revision. The housing market has undershot our
forecasts over the past couple of months. Turnover and financing activity have been coming in
under our earlier expectations. Mortgage loan inquiries have taken another leg down in October after

the September rate hike, after finding something of a level in prior months, with the additional impact
of the November rate hike still to come.
We are also seeing early signs of a turn in the consumer. Consumer income and spending have
so far been quite resilient to pressures from fuel prices and rising interest rates. But our Westpac
Card Tracker is starting to show that discretionary services spending is turning down again in Q4,
joining the discretionary goods category that was already quite weak. We would not over-emphasise

this point, but it does imply that the risks of further surprising resilience are fading.
Subsequent to the November Monetary Policy Board (MPB) meeting, business survey data
has shown weaker conditions and a sharp decline in forward orders. They also show further
evidence that current inflation is mostly a non-labour cost shock that is not being fully
passed through to consumer prices.
As we noted following the November MPB meeting, oil and fuel prices are already materially

lower than the track assumed in the RBA’s forecasts. Dated Brent is nearly one-fourth lower than
on 28 October, the date the RBA’s forecasts were finalised, and spot Brent is around 15% lower. This
significantly lowers the risk of a new wave of pass-through of fuel costs to other prices, as well as
easing consumer inflation perceptions and expectations.
In the media conference following the November meeting, Governor Bullock flagged that it was a

good time to “take stock” of the policy tightening to date. Both we and the market read this as a
signal that the MPB would pause at the December meeting. The 6:3 split vote at the meeting also
suggests that some external members are more attentive to downside risks than the internal
members.
A rate hike in February 2027 cannot be ruled out but it is looking even less likely given signs
in the data and surveys. The MPB will at least discuss a hike in February. The staff and senior

officials remain sensitive to inflation risks and have a downbeat view of supply capacity growth.
Given what we are seeing in the economy now, though, a rate hike in 2027 would require
either a material new inflation shock that has not yet occurred, or would constitute a policy
error on the part of the RBA (something that we will never forecast as a base case, knowing the
talents and capabilities of the staff and the Board). We concur with the dissenting voters in the MPB

that the downside risks need more attention. Policy is clearly restrictive, and the risk of overdoing the
inflation correction is growing. And by the time we get to February, we think there will be more
evidence that the domestic economy is slowing materially, and the inflation outlook with it.

Document 292: Capital Market Outlook (2026-10-13)

### Page 1

CHIEF INVESTMENT OFFICE
Capital Market Outlook

### Page 2

2 of 8 October 13, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
Consumers Feel One Way, but Spend Another
Kirsten Cabacungan, Vice President and Senior Investment Strategist
Americans have spent much of the past several years telling surveys they are cautious
about the economy. Yet they continue to spend as though they are not. The University
of Michigan’s Consumer Sentiment Index remains near multidecade lows, while the
Conference Board’s Index of Consumer Confidence has also been in a yearslong downtrend.

These surveys reflect a list of persistent concerns from elevated food and gas prices to
geopolitical uncertainty and labor market anxiety.
Even so, consumer spending has remained remarkably resilient. The final estimate of Q2
real consumer spending, as measured by PCE, was revised up to an annualized rate of
3.8%, well above the 2.3% 20-year average. More recent data suggest that momentum
has continued. According to Bank of America Institute, the latest monthly total credit and

debit card spending data per household rose 6.2% year-over-year (YoY) in September, and
spending excluding gasoline was up 5.1% YoY, both the third strongest growth rates in the
past four years.1
The persistence of consumer spending is particularly notable given that temporary cyclical
boosts like larger tax refunds are starting to fade. That raises an important question: What is
actually funding strong consumption today? Recent data suggest the drivers are becoming

increasingly more diversified, with broadening labor market trends, healthy consumer
finances and elevated wealth effects all reinforcing one another and underpinning consumers.
First, the labor market continues to provide an important foundation for consumer spending,
and recent trends suggest wage growth is supporting a broader set of households than in
recent years. Jobs growth has slowed from the exceptionally strong pace seen following the
pandemic, but broader indicators suggest labor market conditions remain relatively stable.

Although the September jobs report came in softer than expected, the three- and six-month
rolling averages indicate that the trend in jobs growth likely bottomed out last year and has
gradually improved since (Exhibit 1A). Meanwhile, the unemployment rate remains low by
historical standards and layoffs remain contained. And while wage growth has moderated
and recently trailed headline inflation (Exhibit 1B), cumulative income gains over the past
several years have likely helped many households absorb higher prices better.

An important development though has been improving wage growth trends among lower-
income households, where spending tends to be most sensitive to changes in wages. Bank
of America Institute consumer deposit account data show after-tax wage growth for lower-
income households outpaced higher-income households for a second consecutive month
in August, rising 4.7% YoY compared with 3.5% for higher-income households. Importantly,
this improvement appears to be supporting discretionary spending, not just spending

on necessities. Lower-income discretionary spending rose 5.7% YoY in August, nearly
matching the 5.9% pace for higher-income households. In other words, the narrowing gap
in wage and spending growth across income cohorts suggests consumer resilience looks
to be becoming less dependent on affluent households alone, creating a broader and more
durable foundation for consumption.
Second, household finances remain in relatively good shape, providing consumers with the

flexibility to continue spending. The ratio of household debt to disposable personal income
remains near its lowest level since the late 1990s, excluding the temporary distortions
caused by pandemic-related income support in 2020 and 2021 (Exhibit 1C). Recent
revisions to the Bureau of Economic Analysis national income accounts also suggest
consumers have a larger financial buffer than previous data suggested. Personal saving
estimates over the past year have been revised higher by $320 billion, or 39%, and the

revised savings rate remains above 4% after previous estimates showed it fell to 3% during
the summer.2 Concerns that consumers may be rapidly exhausting their savings buffers
may therefore be overstated. Supporting this view, Bank of America Institute consumer
1 Bank of America Institute, “Consumer Checkpoint: Strength in numbers.” As of October 9, 2026.
2 Bank of America, “Massive Savings Markup = Another Reason to Be Bullish.” As of October 2, 2026.

Investment Implications
Consumer spending strength
remains a key pillar of economic
and earnings growth, which keeps
us constructive on our Equity
overweight. The growing connection
between household wealth and
spending reinforces the importance
of maintaining a diversified portfolio.

### Page 3

3 of 8 October 13, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
deposit account data show that middle- and lower-income households continue to maintain
deposit balances above prepandemic levels across age groups.
Third, elevated household wealth continues to provide a powerful tailwind for consumer
spending, particularly among households that account for an outsized share of overall
consumption. U.S. household net worth rose $12.8 trillion (T) to a record $195.9T in Q2,

driven largely by gains in Equities, which contributed $10.7T of the increase (Exhibit 1D).
As a result, household net worth relative to disposable income climbed to a record 8.3,
meaning households now hold more than eight dollars of wealth for every dollar of annual
disposable income. Households are also sitting on $35.8T in home equity, an all-time high
level that provides a significant balance sheet cushion. Rising asset values have helped to

support spending through what economists refer to as wealth effects. In other words, when
households feel wealthier, they tend to be more willing to spend.
For investors, the key takeaway is that consumer spending is being supported by multiple
engines despite the weaker sentiment data. Labor income trends are supporting wider
participation, healthy balance sheets are helping households absorb economic pressures,
and elevated household wealth continues to sustain higher-income spending. Together,

these forces are supportive of economic growth and corporate profits, which is particularly
important since the consumer accounts for roughly 70% of U.S. gross domestic product.
That said, it does not eliminate risk. A meaningful risk to the consumer would be a scenario
in which both the labor and wealth engines weaken simultaneously. For now, labor market
conditions remain stable, and wealth effects remain supportive. Equity market pullbacks in
recent years have done little to derail consumer spending, suggesting it would likely take a

deeper and more prolonged drawdown to alter consumer behavior.

MARKET VIEW
The Bull Market Turns Four: Older, Unloved—But Still Kicking
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
Ariana Chiu, Assistant Vice President and Investment Strategist
Four years ago this month, virtually no one was in the mood to buy U.S. Equities, and for
good reasons.
Rewind to October 2022: Inflation the month before clocked in at an annual rate of 8.2%.

The yield curve was inverted, a signal that has preceded most past recessions. Consumer
sentiment was abysmal, having dropped to a record low in June 2022. Recession
concerns were mounting, with an October Wall Street Journal poll survey putting the
odds of a recession within 12 months at about 63%. And realizing (belatedly) that
inflation was not “transitory,” the Fed was in the midst of administering one of the most
aggressive monetary tightening cycles in decades, raising the fed funds rate five times

between March and September 2022 by a stunning 300 basis points.
Reflecting the mood at the moment, the S&P 500 closed at 3,577 on October 12, 2022,
down 25% below its January peak, leaving investors battered and bruised but blind to the
fact that the market had bottomed. Four years on, the bull market that emerged from
the gloom of 2022 is still standing and kicking, with the S&P 500 up roughly 130% since
the October 2022 lows.
The more than doubling of the S&P over the past four years has hardly been linear—and

has been accompanied with plenty of pain and doubt. Exhibit 2 maps the rocky path
trodden by the bull over the past four years. Less than six months into the rally, the
markets were rocked by the failure of Silicon Valley Bank (SVB) on March 10, 2023. The
S&P Regional Banks Select Industry Index lost 18% that week and stoked concerns of
massive deposit runs and credit stress across the capital markets. However, owing to
emergency intervention by the government, lower yields and strength elsewhere in the

markets, the crisis passed. Some banks failed that month, but the bull market didn’t.
Exhibit 2: Revisiting the Rocky Path Trodden by the Bull.
Period "Reason to Sell" What happened instead
October 2022 Inflation >8%, aggressive Fed tightening, recession concerns Bull market begins
March 2023 SVB/regional banking crisis Stocks keep climbing
2023-24 Inverted yield curve, “recession inevitable” Economy keeps expanding
2024 “Magnificent 7* bubble,” narrow breadth, expensive valuations Earnings propel market higher

2025 Tariffs, trade war, policy uncertainty Market absorbs another shock
2026 Oil/diesel shock, 5%+ yields, Fed tightening, geopolitical risks Bull reaches fourth birthday
*Apple, Amazon, Alphabet, Nvidia, Meta, Microsoft and Tesla. Source: Chief Investment Office. As of October 9, 2026.
But recessionary concerns hung like threatening clouds over the markets for most of
2023. Aggressive Fed tightening, an inverted yield curve, and soft economic data were

all taken as harbingers of an economic slowdown and profits recession. Yet the S&P 500
continued to climb in 2023, up 26.3% for the year, driven in large part by the outsized
price performance of the Magnificent Seven.
Investors spent much of 2024 fretting about a market too dependent on technology,
and therefore a market too narrow and too expensive. The unwinding of the Japan carry
trade in August 2024 engendered more angst, as did uncertainties around the 2024

U.S. presidential election. But despite a host of misgivings, the S&P 500 delivered total
returns of 25% in 2024. The unloved bull marched on.
Total returns in 2025 were robust as well—roughly 18%—notwithstanding—yet
again—a number of headwinds. In late January, DeepSeek, a cheaper China AI model,
raised questions about America’s technology leadership and the need to spend enormous
sums on chips and data centers. The Nasdaq promptly dropped by 3.1% thereafter.

Investment Implications
The adoption phase of the AI buildout
should translate to broader earnings
growth and performance across
sectors, reinforcing our conviction
in U.S. Equities. Given a strong
fundamental backdrop, we would
continue to buy on weakness as the
bull enters its fifth year

### Page 5

5 of 8 October 13, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
January’s jolt was followed by “Liberation Day,” or the warning of punishing U.S. tariffs
on America’s major trading partners. The trade-policy shock of April 2025 caused the
S&P 500 to drop nearly 19% from its February high to its April 8 low on uncertainty
that higher import costs would decrease corporate margins and consumer spending and
upend global supply chains. Amid the trade tumult, Treasury prices fell and the U.S. dollar

weakened on concerns that foreigners were abandoning U.S. assets. Remember all the
angst over the dollar debasement trade? If that wasn’t enough, expensive valuations and
enormous capital commitments raised doubts about the AI trade and triggered talk of an
AI bubble. Yet despite this wall of worry, the S&P 500, for the third year running, posted
double-digit gains in 2025.
And double-digit gains could be in store this year, with the S&P 500 up 14% through

October 9. The bull has rumbled on, taking in stride the energy shock from the war in
the Middle East, the shift in interest rate expectations from the Fed (from rate cuts
to rate hikes), and the rising uncertainty associated with the midterm elections. These
headwinds have been countered by tailwinds such as better-than-expected corporate
earnings and better-than-anticipated real growth.
Finally, as Exhibit 3 makes clear, what has given this bull so much muscle has been

the U.S. Technology sector, specifically the Magnificent 7. Powering the bull has been
the Magnificent 7, with a staggering 344% return since the 2022 lows, while U.S.
technology has delivered a 277% return versus the S&P 500’s return of 130%. Note the
outperformance of the S&P 500 relative to the main international indices.
Looking forward: Is the bull’s greatest strength also its Achilles’ heel? Bull
markets don’t die of natural causes. And they typically don’t expire because of one

particular signal or indicator. Bull markets turn to bear markets when a cluster of signals/
metrics turn south. Think deteriorating and downward earnings revisions, higher interest
rates, widening credit spreads and tighter lending standards, growth disappointments,
stretched valuations and sentiment, deteriorating market breadth, and an external
shock. Any one of these signals could flash red and not trouble the bull.  But when they
converge or feed off each other, that’s trouble.

In addition, since a great deal of the earnings growth, market leadership and capital
expenditures (capex) spending of this bull market have come from the technology sector,
the bull’s greatest strength could become its greatest vulnerability. AI has become an
investment ecosystem unto itself, and hence the fifth year of the bull market will pivot
in part around AI revenue, frontier model adoption rates, infrastructure spending, free
cash flows, depreciation costs, debt issuance, data center utilization, cloud growth, and

competition via cheaper models and computing alternatives. To put it mildly, there is a
great deal at stake when it comes to the bull and AI.
However, this is where we stand today: We believe the bull will continue to rumble
ahead next year, with AI revenues and productivity gains justifying the costs of the AI
infrastructure. Critically, AI is moving from the buildout phase, dominated by chipmakers
and hyperscaler capex, into the adoption phase, where software firms, healthcare,

finance, energy and a myriad of other firms start to see productivity gains and margin
expansion from rising AI adoption rates. We believe this will give better breadth to the
S&P 500; so too will continued profit growth across various U.S. sectors, AI investment
that starts to pay off, and an economy capable of absorbing setbacks or periodic bouts
of uncertainty.
The path forward won’t be linear—and there will be fits and starts, and market
drawdowns. That’s normal for any market. But that aside, the bull will keep rumbling,

roaming and kicking in 2027.

6 of 8 October 13, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
THE TICKER TAPE
What is our view of the macro and market landscape heading
into 2027 and beyond?
Christopher Hyzy, Chief Investment Office
A supply side-led economic expansion continues, driven by capital investment in the AI
buildout, solid consumer spending and fiscal tailwinds still blowing, albeit with less force.
This creates a backdrop that keeps nominal growth well-above trend and, importantly,

above the interest cost on national debt.
The interest rate backdrop largely remains the same as of today, but, instead of the
sharp increases that we experienced in 2026, the curve should settle down, and yields
should remain relatively range bound. The Fed is likely to tap on the breaks a couple of
more times before reassessing new inflation, growth and employment trends later in
2027.
Concerns surrounding credit risk and "circular" financing are likely to rise the further we

move into the AI buildout, but we do not expect a default cycle to develop. Rather, we
expect the sharp backup in yields to place pressure namely on lower-quality credits in
areas having a difficult time building the cash flow necessary to pay the higher interest
costs.
We expect a broadening out within the market but technology to still maintain its
heavyweight positioning. As we move from training and evolving the large language
models to agentic, and physical AI new leaders are likely to develop. In addition, we

anticipate AI adopters such as biotechnology, transport, logistics, advance manufacturing
and financial companies to enter new growth and productivity phases.
Lastly, overall earnings growth in the U.S. should be low to mid double-digit percent
stages in 2027, which is about half of the growth from 2026 thus far but still around
double from normal levels.
Credit risks developing and higher energy prices for longer are the main risks to our bull
case for Equities extending further into 2027 and beyond.

Index Definitions
ecurities indexes assume reinvestment of all distributions and interest payments. Indexes are unmanaged and do not take into account fees or expenses. It is not possible to
invest directly in an index. Indexes are all based in U.S. dollars.
S&P 500 Index is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States.

University of Michigan’s Consumer Sentiment Index is a monthly survey of consumer confidence levels in the United States conducted by the University of Michigan.
Conference Board’s Index of Consumer Confidence measures what consumers are feeling about their expected financial situation, whether that's optimistic or pessimistic.
Consumer Price Index is a statistical measure that tracks the average change over time in the prices paid by urban consumers for a representative "market basket" of everyday goods and

services.
Nasdaq Composite Index is a stock market measure that tracks the performance of more than 3,000 stocks listed on the Nasdaq exchange.
MSCI Emerging Markets c is a stock market index that tracks the performance of large-cap and mid-cap companies across 24 developing and fast-growing nations.
MSCI All-Country World Index ex USA is a market-capitalization-weighted stock market index that tracks large- and mid-cap equity performance across developed and emerging markets

worldwide, excluding the United States.
MSCI Europe, Australasia, and the Far East (EAFE) Index is a stock market index that measures the performance of large- and mid-cap companies across 21 developed.
Dow Jones Industrial Average Index is a stock market index that tracks 30 major public companies listed on the New York Stock Exchange (NYSE) and Nasdaq.
S&P 500 Equal-Weighted Index is an alternative version of the standard S&P 500 that gives every constituent company an identical weight of 0.2% during each quarterly rebalance, rather

than weighting companies by their market capitalization.
Russell 2000 Index is a stock market index that tracks the performance of approximately 2,000 small-cap (small-capitalization) public companies in the United States.
S&P 500 sub-sectors and industry groups Global Industry Classification Standard (GICS®)/S&P 500 Total Return Index, including Information Technology Total Return (TR) USD;
Consumer Discretionary TR USD; Industrials TR USD; Real Estate TR USD; Communication Services TR USD; Materials  TR USD; Financials TR USD; Consumer Staples TR USD; Utilities TR USD;

Investments have varying degrees of risk. Some of the risks involved with equity securities include the possibility that the value of the stocks may fluctuate in response to events specific to

the companies or markets, as well as economic, political or social events in the U.S. or abroad. Small cap and mid cap companies pose special risks, including possible illiquidity and greater price
volatility than funds consisting of larger, more established companies. Investing in fixed-income securities may involve certain risks, including the credit quality of individual issuers, possible
prepayments, market or economic developments and yields and share price fluctuations due to changes in interest rates. When interest rates go up, bond prices typically drop, and vice versa.

Investments in high-yield bonds (sometimes referred to as “junk bonds”) offer the potential for high current income and attractive total return, but involves certain risks. Changes in economic
conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect a junk bond issuer’s ability to make principal and interest payments. Income from investing in municipal bonds is generally exempt
from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.
Alternative investments are speculative and involve a high degree of risk.
Alternative investments are intended for qualified investors only. Alternative Investments such as derivatives, hedge funds, private credit, private equity funds, and funds of funds can result

in higher return potential but also higher loss potential. Changes in economic conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect your investments. Before you invest in alternative
investments, you should consider your overall financial situation, how much money you have to invest, your need for liquidity, and your tolerance for risk.

Document 341: GPIF、運用姿勢に変化なし ～2026年9月末の運用実績速報、国内債券の比率引き上げ観測は後退か～

Source PDF: all report pdf/GPIF、運用姿勢に変化なし ～2026年9月末の運用実績速報、国内債券の比率引き上げ観測は後退か～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-09
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

Flash Insights

2026年 11月 9日

1/3 
GPIF、運用姿勢に変化なし 
～2026年 9月末の運用実績速報、国内債券の比率引き上げ観測は後退か～ 
 資産運用調査部 主席研究員  奥田 宏二

1. 2026年 7～9月の収益率はマイナス 1.65％ 
年金積立金管理運用独立行政法人（GPIF）は 2026年 11月6日、同年 7～9月の運用実績を公表
した。期間収益率はマイナス 1.65％だった。7～9月は国内外の長期金利が上昇し、債券価格が下
落した。加えて円高も進み、外国資産の円換算額を押し下げた。資産別の収益率は国内債券がマ
イナス 1.80％、外国債券がマイナス 6.52％、国内株式がプラス 3.71％、外国株式がマイナス
1.86％となった。9月末の運用資産額は314兆3,876億円と 6月末から約 3.4兆円減少した。 
GPIFを巡っては、片山さつき財務・金融相が 7月に「日本の金融資産にさらなる投資をしてい
ただくという方向で後押しをする方策を追求したい」と発言し、ポートフォリオに変化があるか
注目が集まっていた。長期金利が上昇していたこともあり、特に国内債への関心が高かった。 
結論から言うと、国内債比率は基本ポートフォリオの 25％に極めて近く、運用姿勢の変化を読
み取ることは難しい。国内債の比率を引き上げるのではないかという観測は一旦後退するだろ
う。

2. 国内債券比率は 25％近辺を維持  
GPIFの基本ポートフォリオは国内外の株式と債券をそれぞれ 25％とし、国内債券には±6ポイ
ントの乖離許容幅が設定されている。GPIFの公表資料によると、年金積立金全体に占める国内債
比率は 2026年9月末時点で 26.49％だった。これには年金特別会計で管理する積立金、約 5.4兆
円が含まれている。 
この積立金を国内債券と年金積立金全体から除き、GPIFの運用資産ベースで計算すると、9月末
の国内債券比率は25.23％となる。6月末の 24.98％から 0.25ポイント上昇したが、基本ポートフ
ォリオの25％をわずかに上回る水準だ（資料1）。 過去の実績と比較しても、今回の比率は突出し
ていない。2020～2025年度の 9月末平均は 25.05％で、2026年9月末はこれを0.18ポイント上回
る程度だ。

### Page 2

2/3 
資料 1 GPIFの運用資産構成比率（推計、2026年 6月末・9月末）

（注）年金特別会計で管理する積立金を除いたGPIF運用資産ベース。 
（出所）GPIF「2026年度第 1四半期運用状況（速報）」、「同第 2四半期運用状況（速報）」より第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成。

3. 国内債券の買越額は推計 1.4兆円  
次に、7～9月の国内債券への買越額を推計する。6月末と 9月末の国内債券残高の変化から、
期間中の運用損益による増減を除いて推計した。 
年金特別会計で管理する積立金を除いた国内債券残高は、6月末の 79兆3,840億円から 9月末
の79兆3,206億円へ、634億円減少した。一方、7～9月の国内債券の運用損益はマイナス 1兆
4,452億円だった。この差額から、国内債券への買越額は約 1.4兆円と推計される（注 1）。4～6
月の約 5.7兆円と比較すると、国内債券への資金配分額は減少した（注2）。 
同じ方法で他の資産への資金配分を試算すると、外国債券は約 3.3兆円の買い越しとなった。
一方、国内株式は約2.0兆円、外国株式は約 0.8兆円の売り越しとなった。

4. 国内債券の運用姿勢に変化はみられず  
2026年7～9月の運用実績をみる限り、GPIFが国内債券の乖離許容幅を積極的に活用し、基本ポ
ートフォリオの25％を上回る水準へと比率を引き上げようとする動きは確認できなかった。 
国内債券は買い越しているとみられるものの、外国債券には国内債券を上回る資金が配分され
ている。4資産の比率はいずれも25％近い。基本ポートフォリオに近づけて運用する従来の姿勢
が維持されていると考えられる。 
以 上

### Page 3

経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。
3/3
【注釈】
1) 買越額は国内債券残高の変化から運用損益を控除した推計値。国内債券には為替ヘッジ付き外
国債券や円建て短期資産が含まれるため、日本国債の純購入額とは必ずしも一致しない。
2) 2026年7～9月は、GPIFの運用資産額の増減から運用収益額を差し引くと、約 1.9兆円の資金
流入があった計算になる。4～6月はほぼゼロだった。こうした資金流入の違いも、国内債券の
買越額を比較する際には考慮する必要がある。

Document 388: TCT Cảng Hàng không Việt Nam - Báo cáo cập nhật 3Q2026 (2026-11-19)

### Page 1

TCT Cảng Hàng không Việt Nam (ACV) 
Áp lực lợi nhuận trong chu kỳ mở rộng công suất 
19/11/2026

KQKD 3Q2026 suy giảm do khấu hao 
và biến động tỷ giá

Lượt khách ACV dự kiến tăng chậm 
hơn toàn ngành do tác động từ Phú 
Quốc và Gia Bình

Long Thành dự kiến khai thác từ cuối 
2026, tạo áp lực lên biên lợi nhuận 
giai đoạn đầu

Duy trì khuyến nghị TRUNG LẬP, giá 
mục tiêu 39,500 VND/cổ phiếu

3Q2026, ACV ghi nhận doanh thu đạt 6,066 tỷ VND (-6% yoy) và LNST công ty mẹ 
đạt 2,206 tỷ VND (-14% yoy). Lợi nhuận suy giảm chủ yếu do (1) khấu hao tăng từ 
các nhà ga mới và (2) ghi nhận lỗ tỷ giá 168 tỷ VND trong kỳ, trái ngược với khoản lãi 
tỷ giá cùng kỳ 2025. Lũy kế 9M2026, doanh thu và LNST công ty mẹ lần lượt đạt 
19,226/7,435 tỷ VND (+0%/+2% yoy).

Chúng tôi dự phóng lượt khách quốc tế/nội địa qua hệ thống ACV tăng trưởng bình 
quân 7%/5.5%/năm đến 2030, thấp hơn toàn ngành do (1) sân bay Phú Quốc 
không còn thuộc hệ thống ACV từ đầu 2026 và (2) một phần khách quốc tế tại Nội 
Bài dịch chuyển tự nhiên sang sân bay Gia Bình khi đi vào khai thác. Mức độ dịch 
chuyển trước năm 2030 dự kiến còn hạn chế do phụ thuộc vào tiến độ hoàn thiện 
sân bay và hạ tầng giao thông kết nối.

Cảng HKQT Long Thành giai đoạn 1 dự kiến khai thác một phần từ cuối tháng 
12/2026 và bắt đầu tiếp nhận các chuyến bay quốc tế đường dài từ Tân Sơn Nhất 
vào ngày 28/03/2027, bổ sung công suất 25 triệu khách/năm cho ACV. Tuy nhiên, 
tốc độ chuyển dịch có thể chậm hơn kế hoạch do hạ tầng kết nối chưa hoàn thiện, 
trong khi chi phí khấu hao và vận hành tăng dự kiến gây áp lực lên biên lợi nhuận 
của ACV giai đoạn 2027–2028.

Sử dụng phương pháp chiết khấu dòng tiền, chúng tôi duy trì khuyến nghị TRUNG 
LẬP đối với ACV với giá mục tiêu 39,500 VND/cổ phiếu, tương đương tiềm năng tăng 
giá 4.5% so với giá đóng cửa ngày 18/11/2026.

Cơ cấu doanh thu 2025 Hoạt động kinh doanh

ACV hiện đang quản lý, đầu tư và khai thác 
hệ thống 21 Cảng hàng không trong cả nước, 
bao gồm 10 Cảng hàng không quốc tế và 11 
Cảng hàng không nội địa. Ngành nghề kinh 
doanh chính của ACV là hoạt động cung cấp 
dịch vụ hỗ trợ trực tiếp cho vận tải hàng 
không, bao gồm dịch vụ hàng không, dịch vụ 
phi hàng không và bán hàng. 
Nguồn: Báo cáo công ty, KBSV

Tăng trưởng bền vững đến từ cả nhóm khách nội địa và quốc tế, tốc độ tăng trung bình dự kiến đạt 
5.5% và 7%/năm giai đoạn 2026-2030. 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo 
Long Thành bổ sung 25 triệu khách/năm từ 2027, song khấu hao tăng mạnh khiến lợi nhuận chạm 
đáy năm 2028 trước khi phục hồi từ 2029. 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo 
Quá trình lên HOSE của ACV tiến triển thuận lợi nhờ các tài sản nhà nước đang dần được tách bạch, 
tạo thuận lợi cho việc hoàn tất cổ phần hóa tài sản khu bay cho phần vốn nhà nước.

HOẠT ĐỘNG KINH DOANH 
KQKD 3Q2026 suy giảm do khấu 
hao và biến động tỷ giá

3Q2026, ACV ghi nhận doanh thu đạt 6,066 tỷ VND (-6% yoy) và LNST công ty 
mẹ đạt 2,206 tỷ VND (-14% yoy). Lợi nhuận suy giảm chủ yếu do (1) khấu hao 
tăng từ các nhà ga mới và (2) ghi nhận lỗ tỷ giá 168 tỷ VND trong kỳ, trái ngược 
với khoản lãi tỷ giá cùng kỳ 2025. Lũy kế 9M2026, doanh thu và LNST công ty 
mẹ lần lượt đạt 19,226/7,435 tỷ VND (+0%/+2% yoy).

Biểu đồ 6. Các quốc gia có lượng khách đến Việt Nam lớn nhất trong 10M2026 (khách) 
Trung Quốc và Hàn Quốc tiếp tục là hai thị 
trường khách quốc tế lớn nhất đến Việt Nam 
trong 10M2026 nhưng tăng trưởng hạn chế 
(+1.4% và -6.3% yoy), chủ yếu chịu tác động 
từ giá nhiên liệu tăng cao, khiến các hãng 
hàng không cắt giảm tần suất bay và du 
khách có xu hướng ưu tiên các điểm đến gần. 
Ngược lại, khách châu Âu tăng mạnh (+51.5% 
yoy), chủ yếu nhờ (1) thị trường Nga phục hồi 
(+150% yoy) sau khi các đường bay thẳng và 
charter được khôi phục, mở rộng và (2) hưởng 
lợi từ chính sách miễn thị thực bổ sung cho 12 
quốc gia châu Âu từ tháng 8/2025 và việc tăng 
cường kết nối hàng không trong năm 2026.

Nguồn: GSO

Triển vọng tăng trưởng hành khách 
tích cực, song ACV dự kiến tăng 
chậm hơn toàn ngành 
 Theo dự báo của Bộ Xây dựng, lượng hành khách thông qua các cảng hàng 
không Việt Nam dự kiến tăng trưởng bình quân 9.7%/năm giai đoạn 2026–
2030, đạt 191.2 triệu lượt vào năm 2030. Về dài hạn, Airbus dự báo lưu lượng 
hành khách hàng không Việt Nam tăng trưởng 7.7%/năm trong 20 năm tới, gần 
gấp khoảng 2 lần mức trung bình toàn cầu (3.9%/năm). Các động lực chính bao 
gồm triển vọng tăng trưởng kinh tế, nhu cầu du lịch gia tăng, chính sách miễn 
thị thực thuận lợi và việc mở rộng mạng đường bay quốc tế, cùng kế hoạch bổ 
sung đội bay của các hãng hàng không.

Chúng tôi dự phóng lượng khách quốc tế và nội địa của ACV tăng trưởng bình 
quân lần lượt 7% và 5.5%/năm đến 2030, thấp hơn mức tăng trưởng chung của 
toàn ngành do (1) sân bay Phú Quốc được chuyển ra khỏi hệ thống ACV từ đầu 
2026 và (2) một phần khách quốc tế tại Nội Bài dịch chuyển tự nhiên sang Gia 
Bình khi sân bay này đi vào khai thác. Chúng tôi đánh giá mức độ dịch chuyển 
trước năm 2030 còn hạn chế do phụ thuộc vào tiến độ xây dựng sân bay và hạ 
tầng kết nối. Bên cạnh đó, đề xuất chuyển các chuyến bay quốc tế từ Nội Bài 
sang Gia Bình hiện chưa được phê duyệt và chưa được phản ánh trong dự 
phóng. Nếu được thông qua, đề xuất này có thể ảnh hưởng đáng kể đến lợi 
nhuận của ACV, do lợi nhuận trên mỗi khách quốc tế cao gấp khoảng 4–5 lần 
khách nội địa, đặc biệt trong giai đoạn sau năm 2030.

Nguồn: ACV, KBSV tổng hợp  Nguồn: ACV, KBSV tổng hợp

Cảng HKQT Long Thành mở ra dư 
địa tăng trưởng dài hạn, song tạo 
áp lực lên biên lợi nhuận trong 
ngắn và trung hạn 
 Cảng HKQT Long Thành dự kiến khai thác một phần từ cuối tháng 12/2026, chủ 
yếu phục vụ các chuyến bay nội địa và một số chuyến quốc tế. Hoạt động 
chuyển các chuyến bay quốc tế đường dài và vận tải hàng hóa từ Tân Sơn Nhất 
dự kiến bắt đầu từ ngày 28/03/2027. Tuy nhiên, chúng tôi đánh giá tốc độ dịch 
chuyển có thể chậm hơn kế hoạch do phụ thuộc vào tiến độ hoàn thiện hạ tầng 
giao thông kết nối với trung tâm TP.HCM, đặc biệt là dự án mở rộng cao tốc 
TP.HCM – Long Thành – Dầu Giây và các dự án kết nối đường sắt bao gồm Thủ 
Thiêm – Long Thành.

Dự án cảng HKQT Long Thành kỳ vọng sẽ mở ra dư địa tăng trưởng dài hạn cho 
ACV, dù gây áp lực lên biên lợi nhuận trong giai đoạn đầu khai thác. Việc đưa 
Long Thành vào vận hành sẽ giúp ACV mở rộng công suất, giảm áp lực quá tải 
tại Tân Sơn Nhất, đồng thời tạo điều kiện thu hút thêm các đường bay quốc tế, 
đặc biệt là đường bay dài và hoạt động vận tải hàng hóa. Tuy nhiên, trong ngắn 
và trung hạn, chi phí khấu hao và vận hành tăng trong khi sản lượng hành 
khách cần thời gian để đạt công suất tối ưu dự kiến sẽ gây áp lực lên biên lợi 
nhuận của ACV giai đoạn 2027–2028. Trong mô hình dự phóng, chúng tôi ghi 
nhận khấu hao Long Thành theo tiến độ nghiệm thu, kết chuyển và đưa vào sử 
dụng từng hạng mục tài sản cố định, thay vì toàn bộ dự án ngay khi bắt đầu vận 
hành.

Nguồn: Báo cáo công ty, KBSV

Chúng tôi khuyến nghị TRUNG LẬP, 
giá mục tiêu 39,500 VND/cổ phiếu  
 Sử dụng phương pháp chiết khấu dòng tiền, chúng tôi duy trì khuyến nghị 
TRUNG LẬP đối với cổ phiếu ACV trong bối cảnh rủi ro từ đề xuất chuyển các 
chuyến bay quốc tế từ Nội Bài sang Gia Bình gia tăng. Giá mục tiêu của ACV là 
39,500 VND/cổ phiếu, tương đương với mức tăng giá tiềm năng 4.5% so với mức 
giá đóng cửa 37,800 VND/cổ phiếu ngày 18/11/2026. Các giả định chính bao 
gồm: 
- Giai đoạn 2026–2030, dự phóng lượt khách quốc tế/nội địa qua hệ 
thống cảng hàng không ACV tăng trưởng bình quân 7%/5.5%/năm, 
thấp hơn tốc độ tăng trưởng chung của ngành hàng không Việt Nam, 
chủ yếu do (1) ACV không còn ghi nhận sản lượng hành khách tại sân 
bay Phú Quốc và (2) giả định một phần khách quốc tế tại Nội Bài dịch 
chuyển tự nhiên sang Gia Bình với mức độ còn hạn chế do hạ tầng kết 
nối chưa hoàn thiện. Dự phóng chưa phản ánh đề xuất chuyển các 
chuyến bay quốc tế sang Gia Bình. 
- Giá dịch vụ trung bình cảng hàng không dự kiến sẽ điều chỉnh tăng 2% 
mỗi 4 năm. 
- Dự án Long Thành giai đoạn 1 và giai đoạn 2 bắt đầu đưa vào khai thác 
từ cuối 2026 và đầu 2032, gia tăng doanh thu nhưng đồng thời tạo áp 
lực lên biên lợi nhuận trong giai đoạn đầu vận hành do chi phí khấu 
hao và vận hành tăng.

### Page 8

Rủi ro giảm đối với giá mục tiêu ACV chủ yếu đến từ khả năng đề xuất chuyển 
các chuyến bay quốc tế từ Nội Bài sang Gia Bình được phê duyệt. Kịch bản cơ 
sở hiện chỉ phản ánh mức dịch chuyển tự nhiên. Trong trường hợp đề xuất 
được thông qua, sản lượng khách quốc tế qua hệ thống ACV có thể thấp hơn 
đáng kể so với dự phóng, kéo theo suy giảm doanh thu và lợi nhuận do khả 
năng sinh lời trên mỗi khách quốc tế cao hơn khoảng 4–5 lần so với khách nội 
địa.

Biểu đồ 13. P/B, ROE lịch sử

P/B của ACV tiếp tục giảm về quanh 1.8–1.9x, 
chạm và có lúc xuống dưới ngưỡng -2std. Đây 
là vùng thấp nhất 5 năm và chiết khấu sâu so 
với mức trung bình 5 năm, phản ánh lo ngại 
về áp lực khấu hao và chi phí khi Long Thành 
đi vào vận hành.

Document 397: The US tariff wall keeps changing, but it isn’t coming down

Source PDF: all report pdf/The US tariff wall keeps changing, but it isn’t coming down.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-25
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

The US is preparing another round of tariﬀs, this time targeting
structural overcapacity across 16 trading partners. It is another
reminder that while the legal justiﬁcation for tariﬀs keeps
changing, US protectionism is becoming increasingly diﬃcult to
unwind
Inga Fechner
The US is preparing new tariﬀs targeting structural overcapacity. Legal routes
may change, but the US tariﬀ wall is proving hard to dismantle
If anyone thought the Supreme Court ruling earlier this year

marked the beginning of the end of the US tariﬀ wall, think again.
The Trump administration is reportedly preparing new tariﬀs
following its Section 301 investigation into structural excess
capacity. China is the obvious target, but the list of 16 economies
also includes the European Union, Japan, South Korea, Mexico, India
and several Southeast Asian manufacturing hubs. The rates and
exact product coverage remain unknown, but the direction does
not.

### Page 2

We've argued for some time that tariﬀs are here to stay. In our
2025 trade analysis, we expected that even successful trade
negotiations and legal challenges would not signiﬁcantly bring
down the overall tariﬀ level, as Washington had other legal
instruments available. And that is pretty much what happened.
When the Supreme Court struck down tariﬀs imposed under IEEPA,
Washington initially turned to Section 122 as a temporary solution
before replacing it with broader Section 301 tariﬀs in July. Section

232 tariﬀs have also expanded considerably, with a growing
number of product groups now facing additional duties ranging
from 10% to as much as 100%, and more could follow.
Investigations into anthracite coal and medical equipment remain
open, while robotics, industrial machinery and wind turbines have
already moved to the negotiation stage, with tariﬀs still on the
table.
Section 232 tariffs already cover major
industries
Tariﬀ ranges reﬂect diﬀerences by product and/or country of origin.

Minimum import prices for polysilicon and derivatives will also apply
from 4 December 2026.
Source: Congressional Research Service, R49481; White House Presidential
Actions

### Page 3

In turn, there is no uniform tariﬀ, but rather a complex system of
country- and sector-speciﬁc duties based on diﬀerent legal
authorities. According to the ETRX US Eﬀective Tariﬀ Rate Index,
which is based on US Census Bureau trade data, the eﬀective tariﬀ
rate stood at 6.8% for all US imports in September. For imports from
China, it was around 21%, compared with 6.6% for imports from
the EU.
That gap partly reﬂects the much broader range of tariﬀs on

Chinese goods, including Section 301 duties dating back to US
President Donald Trump's ﬁrst administration. Both China and the
EU are also exposed to newer Section 301 tariﬀs and relevant
sector-speciﬁc Section 232 measures. The next overcapacity tariﬀs
would add another legal instrument to this increasingly complex
tariﬀ system, although it remains unclear how they would interact
with current tariﬀs and agreed country-speciﬁc caps. According to
Reuters, Washington is expected to respect the 15% tariﬀ cap

agreed with the EU, while the 20% cap on Trump's second-term
tariﬀs on China would remain in place as long as the current US-
China truce is extended beyond 10 January 2027.
Overcapacity: the next justiﬁcation
Structural overcapacity is the latest justiﬁcation, and it is deﬁned
broadly. USTR points not only to subsidies, but also to state-owned
enterprises, subsidised lending, market-access barriers, wages,
currency practices and environmental and labour standards. And

the potential sectoral reach is equally broad, stretching from steel,
cars and batteries to semiconductors, machinery, chemicals and
solar panels. That gives Washington considerable room to
introduce new tariﬀs.
Another legal fallback?
And another legal route for tariﬀs could matter more than ever. The
10-12.5% Section 301 tariﬀs introduced in July against 60 trading
partners over their failure to impose and eﬀectively enforce
prohibitions on imports of goods produced with forced labour are

being challenged before the US Court of International Trade.
Plaintiﬀs argue, among other things, that the measures are too

The court has heard verbal arguments, and a ruling remains
pending.
We don't know how that case will end, and importantly, the two

investigations were launched almost simultaneously in March –
overcapacity on 11 March and forced labour on 12 March. But with
the forced-labour tariﬀs now facing a legal challenge, the
overcapacity investigation could oﬀer Washington another route to
maintain part of the tariﬀ wall should the current tariﬀs be struck
down or narrowed.
Different politics, same direction
Democrats taking control of Congress in January will put greater
pressure on Trump's tariﬀs. In theory, Congress has considerable

power over trade. It could try to restrict presidential tariﬀ authority,
remove individual tariﬀs and use hearings and investigations to
scrutinise the administration's trade policy. In practice, though,
actually reversing Trump's tariﬀs will be diﬃcult. Congress has
delegated substantial powers to the executive under laws such as
Sections 232 and 301. Curtailing those powers would require
legislation, which Trump could veto. Without the two-thirds

majority needed to override a presidential veto, Democrats' ability
to dismantle the tariﬀ wall will remain limited.
Either way, we don't think the blue wave will mark a return to free
trade. The Biden years already showed that tariﬀs in strategically
important sectors enjoy support across political camps.
Protectionism may become more targeted, but neither it nor the
tariﬀ wall is likely to disappear.
